# Aster family — measured review of five repositories (2026-10-26)

**What this is.** A read-only, measured review of hudsonclavin-cloud/{asteroid-mining-planner, skyhook, Partforge, Idea-Bank, suborbital-leg-cost-model} run on 2026-10-26. Each live repo was reviewed by one agent that ran the repo's own test suites and read its code and docs, then re-checked by an adversarial second agent whose job was to refute the first; the five verified cards were synthesized into one cross-repo picture. Every count below was produced by a command on that date; file:line citations are the agents'. Labels follow the house rule: [Certain] / [Likely] / [Speculative].

**How to read the completion figures.** They are estimates toward each repo's *own* definition of done, which differs wildly (Aster's product-vision v1 is met; Skyhook's "done" is a funded two-year Phase 0 exit). They are not grades. Where a repo never defines done (PartForge) the label is [Speculative] by construction.

**Deviation note.** Running test suites is reserved for another role in Aster's AGENTS.md §3; it was done here under the owner's request for a measured review, read-only, with `git status` clean afterwards in every repo. No repo file was changed by the review; the only writes are on branch `aster-family` of the hub.

**Corrections to the first family scaffold (same day).** The hub's first draft, written before the review, stated four sibling facts from inference that the review refuted: Skyhook was called public with a Pages site (it is private, `has_pages: false`); its verification memo was cited as VM-001/VM-002 (the repo copy holds VM-001 only); Idea-Bank was credited with 97 test files (98); and a Pages deploy run was read as "Pages enabled". All four are fixed on the branch, and the lesson is structural: sibling facts are now generated by `tools/family/family-index.mjs`, never typed.

## 2.1 Aster (asteroid-mining-planner)


**Scope line (verbatim from the verified card):** hudsonclavin-cloud/asteroid-mining-planner (local: /home/user/asteroid-mining-planner; verified against main = origin/main = 62ddb17; working tree on branch aster-family @ f077390 with pre-existing ' M .dispatch-scope'; 2026-10-26)

**One line.** A deployed, oracle-validated browser asteroid-mission planner plus an npm MCP server, wrapped in an unusually rigorous honesty-and-governance culture — the planner v1 the repo originally defined is built and byte-reproducible, but the MCP half of 'one core, two interfaces' has not compiled since 2026-10-14, CI on main went red for a day on 2026-10-24 because the rewritten history orphaned a pinned commit, the control-plane docs are a month stale, and the active Slice 19.5 is roughly a third done.

**What it is.** Aster is a static GitHub-Pages web app (Preact + TypeScript + Three.js, Vite build committed to docs/ and deployed by GitHub's 'pages build and deployment' on every push to main) that lets someone screen 41,906 near-Earth asteroids for reachability: a 3D solar-system view with the NEA cloud, a porkchop planner (clean-room Izzo Lambert solver, C3, ΔV stack, declination-of-launch-asymptote feasibility, delivered-mass cost card against 38 sourced launch-vehicle anchors), a multi-target compare page, and an About/validation page. The same src/v2/core math is compiled into aster-mission-mcp@0.1.0, a stdio MCP server whose answers are evidence envelopes (value, units, confidence class, provenance commits) and whose out-of-envelope answers are structured refusals. The repo also holds a pre-registered, DOI-sealed study (Slice 16, DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21752617 at src/v2/SLICE_16_FOUNDING.md:1337) of whether LLM agents relay that evidence faithfully (468 runs; 23.8% / 32.5% full faithfulness, STATUS.md:51). Its audience today is reviewers rather than paying users — the master plan marks the flagship 'Buyer today? No [Certain, ~]' (strategy/ASTER_FAMILY_MASTER_PLAN.md:54) while naming Traffic as 'the nearest real market' (:58). Work is organized in numbered slices with additive-only founding documents, DEC/OQ ids, hook-enforced staging rules and a dispatch model in which Hudson alone pushes (AGENTS.md:103). [Certain] for all of the above from the cited files.

**Definition of done (the repo's own).** Three layers, none a single 'v1 complete' flag. (1) Legacy V1: FOUNDING_DOCUMENT.md:241 '## 11. Definition of Done' (:244 '500+ NEAs', :246 'Paper Value AND Realizable NPV') — superseded; that runtime is frozen at /legacy/ (LEGACY.md:88 'Slice 19.5 quarantine'; DEC-19.5-1 at src/v2/SLICE_19_5_FOUNDING.md:104). (2) V2 architecture: V2_FOUNDING_DOCUMENT.md:705 'A slice is not complete when it looks good. A slice is complete when it clears numeric bars' — done is per-slice cutover bars in each SLICE_*_FOUNDING.md. (3) Product v1: ASTER_PRODUCT_VISION.md (repo ROOT, not strategy/; :3 'Written 2026-05-15') defines the mission planner as Slices 10-14 (:44) and estimates 'complete v1 to late 2026 or early 2027' (:58); STATUS.md:38-45 records Slices 9-18 COMPLETE/CLOSED/PUBLISHED, so that v1 was met by July-September 2026. strategy/ASTER_ROADMAP_2026-08.md:22 'Slice 20 is not a terminus.' The operative 'done' today is Slice 19.5's exit: src/v2/SLICE_19_5_FOUNDING.md:367-371 (§8: Fronts A–F shipped or cut per §5, G shipped or cut with disclosure, founding doc LOCKED; 40–48 h, 5–6 weeks at 8 h/week) and §7 :354-365 (npm run verify N/N green; MCP test and eval green; CI green on every pushed commit; all 326 findings dispositioned; fresh Linux clone installs working hooks).

**Completion: 82% [Likely].** Two frames. (a) Against the repo's product-level v1 (ASTER_PRODUCT_VISION.md Slices 10-14 plus the MCP surface the project later made load-bearing): capability is built, deployed and reproducible — I re-measured tsc exit 0 (3.2 s), 97/97 test files / 336 pass / 0 fail / 1 skipped / 0 load-timeouts (375.8 s, Node 22.22.0), and a scratch rebuild of HEAD byte-identical to committed docs/ in all 51 files; GitHub Actions confirms CI green and a Pages deploy at 62ddb17 on 2026-10-25. What keeps it from 100: the project redefined the product as the honesty contract (README.md:1; Slice 19/19.5), and that contract is measurably broken on the agent surface — `cd mcp && npm test` fails to compile at mcp/src/tools/compute-shared.ts:262 (TS2322, reproduced; introduced by 4535df9 on 2026-10-14, which touched no mcp/ file; ci.yml has no mcp job), the published 0.1.0 (2026-07-10) lacks fidelity tiers (no L0/L1/L2 handling anywhere in mcp/src) and the nearZeroC3 field (present in 5 mcp/src files, never republished), the cost card certifies GREEN cells the L1 tier voids (no tier/encounter reference in src/v2/app/porkchop/main.ts:419-548), and all four rolling ephemeris series ended JD 2461329.5 = 2026-10-16. Two facts the first reviewer lacked lower it a further ~3: CI on main was red at 361ddba (2026-10-24, full-tests job) because the rewritten history orphaned the b1075f3 pin — the predicted fragility has already fired — and the tracked tree is 425.6 MB with a 50.5 MB JSON committed twice. (b) Against Slice 19.5's own exit (§8): Phase 0 and Fronts A+B shipped (s19-5-runs/ab-REPORT.recovered.md 'RUN: COMPLETE', fix2-REPORT.md 'tip 5e880e0, VERIFY 336/336'; §9.1 ruling record landed in 361ddba on 2026-10-24 though the doc dates the lock 2026-10-14); Fronts C–G unshipped; A+B ≈10 of 40–48 h → ~25-35%. Headline 82 is frame (a); frame (b) ≈30. [Likely] because 'done' is a moving target by design.

### Strengths

- Measured honesty is real: a scratch rebuild of HEAD reproduces all 51 deployed build files byte-for-byte; tsc is clean; 97/97 files / 336 tests pass under a runner that asserts its own accounting (tools/run-tests.mjs:11,93-94); the golden-numbers job pins production outputs (tests/v2-golden/launch-vehicles.golden.test.mjs:1-11, :85-89); GitHub Actions corroborates (CI + Pages green at 62ddb17) [Certain].
- The math core is small, legible and oracle-validated; DLA frame established by measurement with the rejected rotation recorded (INVARIANTS.md:59-62); poliastro oracle-only (INV-024) [Certain].
- Evidence envelopes + structured refusals are a differentiating idea for agent consumers (INV-027/INV-032 at INVARIANTS.md:201,206), and the project tested its own premise with a DOI-sealed pre-registration and reported weak results honestly (STATUS.md:51-57) [Certain].
- Self-correction culture: errors found by the project itself are recorded additively (L1 boundary 2,352-body over-claim at STATUS.md:312; 30 s black-screen hypothesis measured false at SLICE_19_FOUNDING.md:73-74; per-degree dogleg figure unsourced, 1d6ffe8) [Certain].
- Governance that scales to agents and a 326-finding audit fully dispositioned with rulings; run evidence committed (s19-5-runs/) including the admission that a batch log was lost (SLICE_19_5_FOUNDING.md:433-436) [Certain].
- Licensing hygiene: MIT LICENSE with SPDX id, NOTICE attributing Izzo/poliastro/Tycho-2, mcp/LICENSE in the npm 'files' list; no secrets in history as far as pattern sweeps show [Certain].
- Cross-repo dependency done right once: the K2 prototype snapshots Skyhook's rev_a.json with sha256 + source commit and a verify gate; today's live Skyhook file still matches [Certain].

### Weaknesses

- The honesty contract is enforced by prose and process, not by CI, exactly where agents consume it: mcp/ is outside the CI matrix, so a two-line signature change broke the agent surface on 2026-10-14 while 97 test files stayed green [Certain].
- CI on main is structurally fragile: the suite depends on a commit (b1075f3) that exists only on side branches, and it already failed once (361ddba, 2026-10-24) before fetch-depth: 0 papered over it [Certain].
- Documentation mass exceeds code mass and is decaying: 231 markdown files / ~42k lines vs ~22k lines of src/v2 TypeScript; STATUS.md (559 lines, 83 KB) has a month-stale header; AGENTS.md is protected so known-false lines persist; the Slice 19.5 founding doc says 'DRAFT — not locked' at :3 and 'LOCKED' at :441 [Certain].
- History rewrite (2026-10-12) without re-pinning: main's 50 commits have no merge base with the 925-commit line that tests, eval reports, STATUS and the prior review's own citations (e70c00b/59a7dd9) reference [Certain].
- Repository weight: 425.6 MB tracked, with 50.5 MB and 32.9 MB JSONs each committed twice and no LFS — slow clones, a GitHub size warning, and a Pages origin serving a 50 MB asset [Certain].
- First-contact UX contradicts the thesis: expired-ephemeris warning on the landing page, GREEN delivered mass beside a voiding caveat, no reachability sort, 35.5 MB transfer [Certain on facts; Likely on reviewer impact].
- Velocity is falling with the semester: 236 commits in 2026-05 vs 57 in 09 and 53 in 10, and only three push events reached main in October (10-14, 10-24, 10-25 by CI runs); the active slice is 40-48 h of mostly governance work [Certain on counts].
- Dead and duplicated surface: proxy/ and worker/ byte-identical (diff -rq empty); DATA_SOURCES.md:3-5 describes only the legacy worker pipeline as 'current'; LEGACY.md:10-11 and proxy/README.md:44 link absolute macOS paths; src/v2/research/ folder names (slice-14-composition … slice-20-mission-planner-ux) describe a reassigned May plan [Certain].
- No buyer for the flagship by the plan's own table (ASTER_FAMILY_MASTER_PLAN.md:54); the one lens with 'YES' buyers (Traffic, :58) is off-brand and un-chartered [Certain on text].

### Hidden assumption

That the honesty layer — envelopes, disclosures, additive founding docs, refusals — is the product and the moat, and that one person plus agent dispatches can keep it consistent across a web app, an npm package, a 231-file document corpus and a deployed site by process rather than by machines. The evidence it is weak is now stronger than the first card showed: the one surface agents consume (mcp/) has been uncompilable since 2026-10-14 and unpublished since 2026-07-10 with 97 green test files not noticing; CI on main itself went red on 2026-10-24 for a history-hygiene reason no test could catch; the living state file is a month wrong; hooks do not install. Slice 19.5 names the gap (§1 'honesty rules exist as copies scattered across surfaces') but routes the fix through more process (Fronts C-G) rather than gates that fail. A second assumption — that Fellows/employers/investors reward verifiable honesty over polish — is plausible for the stated audience and unproven; the plan concedes the flagship has no buyer [Likely weak].

### Offers to other repos

- EvidenceEnvelope + structured-refusal schema (mcp/src/envelope/, mcp/src/tools/envelope-schema.ts; INV-027/INV-032 at INVARIANTS.md:201,206): a machine-readable form of the [Certain]/[Likely]/[Speculative] convention Skyhook and suborbital-leg-cost-model already use in prose.
- Snapshot-with-hash dependency pattern (tools/k2-momentum-research/build-snapshots.mjs + verify.mjs on the k2 branch): copy the upstream parameter file verbatim, record sha256 + source commit, fail a gate on drift — what suborbital-leg-cost-model's INVARIANTS 'Upstream anchors (skyhook repo)' asks for.
- Repo hygiene tooling: .githooks/pre-commit (dispatch-scope allowlist + --numstat additive-only guard :50-67) and pre-push; tools/run-tests.mjs per-file watchdog with accounting identity; the golden-numbers CI pattern (tests/v2-golden). Lesson to carry with them: ship hooks as 100755 with a `prepare` script, and put every compiled surface (here mcp/) in CI.
- Founding-doc lifecycle and templates (.claude/skills/slice-discipline, dispatch-writer, multi-agent-audit; AGENTS.md §6) — the STATUS/INVARIANTS/DEVLOG + additive decision-record pattern suborbital-leg-cost-model already copied.
- Astrodynamics core as a package: src/v2/core (Izzo Lambert, Keplerian propagation, UTC→TDB, frames) ships inside aster-mission-mcp's dist/src; Skyhook's tools/sims and the K2 model could consume it instead of re-deriving — once the mcp build is fixed and 0.2.0 ships.
- Data: 41,906-body NEA catalog with per-body minimum outbound v∞ (docs/lambert-screen-cache.json, gzip 8.83 MB), Horizons fixtures 2026-2040 (5,479 daily records), launch-vehicle curves with 38 'official'-basis anchors (src/v2/porkchop/launch-vehicles.ts).
- Deploy pattern: committed docs/ as the Pages artifact with build-reproducibility as the deploy proof (AGENTS.md §9), confirmed working via Actions 'pages build and deployment' — the same shape a family index page could reuse; MIT LICENSE + NOTICE as the family's licensing template.

## 2.2 Skyhook


**Scope line (verbatim from the verified card):** hudsonclavin-cloud/skyhook (/home/user/skyhook; local HEAD 34935cb = remote main, 2026-09-10, 50 commits, 88 tracked files; PRIVATE repo; remote additionally holds 6 unmerged claude/* branches with 46 unique commits pushed 2026-10-22/23 and a fully-merged overnight-20260702 branch)

**One line.** A disciplined, test-locked Phase 0 paper study of a 2.5 km/s rotovator whose own finance model says the base case does not close, whose canon re-derives to four figures (with one internal radius inconsistency), and whose most urgent unrecorded fact is that 46 agent-authored commits fixing several of its known defects sit on six unmerged, un-CI'd branches that no document in the repo mentions.

**What it is.** Skyhook is a design-and-economics study for a rotating momentum-exchange tether at 610 km that catches suborbital payloads at ~4.59 km/s ground-relative and throws them to GTO/high-elliptical orbit at a 2.5 km/s tip, with 2.8 km/s cislunar and 3.5 km/s Mars-escape tiers kept as labeled options (params/rev_a.json:22-57). On main it is Python plus static HTML: a physics module implementing EQ-1..7 (physics/skyhook_physics.py, 305 lines), a 17-node planar tether-dynamics model with a JS port cross-checked to 1.6e-10 (physics/tether_dynamics.py, tools/twin/), a workbook-reconciled finance model plus eight analysis modules and eight FINDINGS memos (finance/), six browser simulators (tools/sims/), and a 74-item pytest suite that passes in 6 s. On the remote, six Claude-authored branches (2026-10-22/23) add ~12,000 lines: a canon-claim registry and drift linter (canon/), catch kinematics, a momentum-draw ledger, a rotating-frame modal 'stethoscope', a leg make-or-buy ledger, three new sim pages, and a PM-001 study of Skyhook as an asteroid-probe launcher that pulls Aster mission cells. It is for Hudson first; the repo is private (GitHub API visibility: private), so today no professor or investor can see it. Nothing has been built or tested physically, and no external party has replied to any outreach (docs/outreach_log.md has no entry after 2026-08-31).

**Definition of done (the repo's own).** The repo defines done at the program level, not the repo level. Phase 0 'Foundations' exit criteria (docs/project_instructions.md:179-184): 'simulation validated against historical flight-tether data; joint strength statistics in hand; capture demo video that does the fundraising for us', with deliverables of a full digital twin, a material qualification campaign and handbook, a grapple ground demo and regulatory groundwork, budget class $10-30M over Years 0-2. The nearer, implicit 'this study is finished' definition is handoff.md's six open items (handoff.md:29-35): rebuild the frontier on the finance model; NC State research proposal; propagate the creep finding to DECISION_LOG (done 2026-09-02, docs/DECISION_LOG.md:66); backfill claude.ai-only docs VM-002/RB-001/TS-001/RN-001/OP-001; run ST-001/ST-002; apply the §4.1 wording (done 2026-09-02, docs/project_instructions.md:111). README.md:76-86 adds fiber vendor quote, rocket-leg pricing, named anchor customer, primary PDFs into docs/references/ (listed at README.md:51 but the directory does not exist), community engagement. There is no tag, milestone, STATUS file or PR that names a repo-level 'done', and nothing in the repo records the existence of the six remote branches.

**Completion: 12% [Likely].** Against the repo's own Phase 0 exit (docs/project_instructions.md:184): (1) 'simulation validated against historical flight-tether data' - the dynamics model is planar, 17 nodes, undamped by default (physics/tether_dynamics.py:242), with a [Speculative] 200 t hub (:75-77) and no YES2/SEDS/TSS reference anywhere in physics/, tests/ or tools/twin/ (grep); it is self-consistent (V1-V4 pass; JS port agrees to 1.6e-10) but not validated. The unmerged SH-001 modal analysis and CW-001 catch kinematics add self-consistency, not validation. ~15% of that criterion. (2) 'joint strength statistics in hand' - zero data points; docs/st001_splice_test.md is a protocol, no results file on main or any branch. 0%. (3) 'capture demo video' - nothing. 0%. (4) Material campaign, grapple prototype, regulatory engagement - one 2-page conjunction PDF. Weighted, 10-12% of Phase 0 exit. Against the softer 'self-consistent paper study' framing: main is ~65% (physics and economics tested and reconciled, 73 passed / 1 skipped measured; frontier still shows retracted numbers; five decision docs outside the repo), and main-plus-branches is ~75% (DEVIATIONS #4 and #8 fixed, pytest-10 debt fixed, canon linter added, all six branch suites green locally). I report 12 because the repo chose the Phase 0 criterion and never redefined done, and because unmerged work is not shipped.

### Strengths

- Canon discipline that actually works: one JSON file, tests that fail if a number drifts (tests/test_rev_a_physics.py:171-185 re-derive all five mass-ratio anchors), a CI self-check, and an independent re-derivation by this review matching every mass-ratio, C3, apogee and tip-g value to 4 sig figs [Certain, measured]
- Error honesty as a habit: DECISION_LOG.md:49-53 states plainly that a prior 'robust' claim mixed an optimistic launch price into the conservative column, with a regression test guarding the re-mix; handoff.md:21-24 retracts a 67-73% margin claim and names the cause ('quoting canon from memory') [Certain]
- Reconciliation gates before findings: rightsize_sensitivity.py, demand_sensitivity.py and export_grid.py refuse to report until they reproduce the tested anchors; the gate caught a spec error in its own dispatch (DECISION_LOG.md:53) [Certain]
- Provenance envelope on the exported grid: schema_version, git_commit, md5 of rev_a.json and the workbook canonicalised across line endings (export_grid.py:97-113), caveats carried verbatim and asserted by tests, interpolation fidelity measured at export; the 2026-09-09 CI failure was exactly this envelope catching a CRLF drift, fixed by 34935cb [Certain]
- Cross-language verification of the digital twin: Python golden run vs JS port agree to 1.571e-10 in position and ~1e-13 in energy drift (measured); the JS loader throws rather than falling back without the canonical file (tools/twin/params-loader.js:27,36,54) [Certain]
- Economics stated against interest: materials/core.md:107 and README say the conservative case does not close; the demand pressure test shows the named shortlist cannot close it at any price tested (outreach_log.md:61-66) [Certain]
- The unmerged branch work extends the discipline rather than eroding it: a canon-claim registry and drift linter with a ratchet baseline (canon/), per-tool canon snapshots guarded by tests (tests/test_tool_canon_snapshots.py), and xfail markers that document rather than hide the com_radius_km inconsistency; all six branch suites pass locally [Certain, measured]
- Fast, green, dependency-light test suite on main (73 pass in ~6 s; pytest + openpyxl only) [Certain, measured]

### Weaknesses

- The repo's definition of done (Phase 0 exit) is a $10-30M, two-year, team-and-lab program; the repo never defines a reachable v1 for a solo weekend builder, so progress has no finish line it can hit [Certain]
- 46 commits of agent-authored work (noreply@anthropic.com) landed on six sibling branches in ~32 hours with no PR, no CI run and no mention in handoff.md, README.md or DECISION_LOG.md; the repo's own 'repo = single source of truth' rule (handoff.md:42) is now violated by the repo's own remote [Certain]
- The 'digital twin' is a 17-node planar undamped model with a [Speculative] hub mass and a target-strain-derived modulus; it has never been compared to YES2/SEDS/TSS flight data, which is the exit criterion it exists to meet (physics/tether_dynamics.py:27-33, :75-77, :242) [Certain]
- Five load-bearing decision documents live outside the repo (handoff.md:33); program_manifest.md:32 and aster-family/MANIFEST.json:74 describe them as present [Certain]
- The frontier still shows retracted numbers (frontier.html:105), 50 days after the grid it should read was exported and tested, and none of the six new branches touched it [Certain]
- The finance ladder's assumptions are read from a binary .xlsx (skyhook_finance.py:104-120); the workbook is 'human-review-pending' (:11) and not diffable, so the most-disputed inputs are the least reviewable [Certain]
- Research inputs that name hard parts (HMPE creep, splice retention, insurance rates) are Perplexity-surfaced and flagged 'spot-check' (MP-001 and LR-001 headers); nothing has been spot-checked since 31 Aug [Certain]
- Outreach produced zero logged responses in 16 weeks and the log stopped recording after 31 Aug, breaking its own 'log silence as data' rule (outreach_log.md:33) [Certain]
- Doc rot in the first file anyone reads: README.md:51 lists a docs/references/ directory that does not exist, :67 a YOUR_USERNAME clone URL, :52 a regulatory folder with content that is not there; unchanged on every branch [Certain]
- Founding document carries an unedited drafting artifact: docs/project_instructions.md:44 '...dips to ~290 km altitude at bottom of swing... wait — for an atmosphere-grazing design we want the tip much lower.' [Certain]
- The canonical file disagrees with itself: rev_a.json:10 com_radius_km 6988 implies R_E 6378 while every derived kinematic value and physics/skyhook_physics.py:40 use 6371; a professor who checks sqrt(mu/R) from the file's own fields gets 7.5525, not the module's 7.5563 [Certain]
- The repo is private, has no LICENSE, no root index.html, Pages is off (has_pages false), and 46/50 main commits are under a misspelled email - so the 'portfolio' purpose is currently unserved by construction, not just by hygiene [Certain]
- The 'mandatory' confidence-label rule (CLAUDE.md) is unevenly applied: 109/57/59 tags, but 24 .py/.md files including handoff.md, DEVIATIONS.md, five FINDINGS memos and four finance modules carry none [Certain]
- A placeholder test that always skips (tests/test_analysis_layer.py:13) survives on every branch; the pytest-10 fixture deprecation is fixed only on the unmerged branches [Certain]
- Most code and prose was generated by overnight/dispatched agent runs (MORNING_REPORT.md; DECISION_LOG.md:81; the Oct 22-23 branches); the portfolio value depends on Hudson defending every equation himself, and the gap between agent-authored depth and owner-reviewed depth widened by ~12,000 lines in two days [Likely]

### Risks

| Split source of truth (claude.ai project vs repo) produces another canon error or another unintended external contact | High - it has happened three times (3.5 as reference; 2.8/3.5 conflation; Impulse contacted despite deferral), VM-002/RB-001/TS-001/RN-001/OP-001 are still outside the repo, and the remote branches add a second split (repo main vs repo branches) | High - credibility is the program's only capital | [Certain] that the split exists; [Likely] that it recurs |
| HMPE creep at SF 2.5 invalidates the reference design point | Medium - the guidance is secondary-sourced but consistent across sources (MP-001 §3c, LR-001 §D) | Very high - SF 5 moves the mass ratio from ~122x toward ~800x and every $/kg figure by ~6x; the repo itself calls this 'a refutation of the design, not a variant of it' (DECISION_LOG.md:72) | [Likely] |
| The business never closes as modeled because demand is 3-10x smaller than the capacity-sized cost base needs | High on current evidence - named shortlist 24-56 t/yr vs 152-242 t/yr minimum viable (RIGHTSIZE_FINDINGS.md:8,45) | High - but survivable if the NRE line is attacked (procure the leg); the unmerged leg-make-or-buy branch is the first attempt | [Certain within the model] |
| Agent-authored depth exceeds owner-defensible depth | Medium-high - raised from the reviewer's 'Medium': ~12,000 lines of modal analysis, momentum ledgers and a canon linter were produced in ~32 hours; nothing in the repo shows Hudson's own derivations or review notes | High in an interview or professor meeting; low otherwise | [Likely] |
| Simulators and the synced skill drift from canon and are what an outsider actually opens | High on main (theater 3.5 km/s, site LAD bug, SKILL.md 440x unqualified, Aster founding draft 3.50 km/s); Medium after merging the base branch (theater and site fixed; skill and Aster file still wrong) | Medium - visible surfaces contradict tested surfaces | [Certain] |
| Workbook-as-assumption-source hides changes to the disputed inputs | Medium - md5 provenance detects change but not what changed | Medium - a silent edit to Assumptions row 5 would propagate to every FINDINGS memo | [Likely] |
| Hygiene debts bite at the moment the repo goes public: no LICENSE, misattributed commits (46 gmauil + 46 noreply@anthropic.com), README rot, stale handoff | Certain if the repo is flipped public as-is | Low-medium individually; collectively they are the first things a skeptical reader sees | [Certain] |

### Hidden assumption

The project rests on the assumption that its hard constraint is economics (NRE, demand, launch price) rather than materials - specifically that a Zylon/HMPE tether can run indefinitely at SF 2.5, i.e. at 28-40% of rated break strength under a load that is genuinely sustained in the rotating frame. Every tested number in the repo (121.83x, 609 t, $8.7B, Y17) sits on that design point. The repo's own research (DECISION_LOG.md:66-78, MP-001 §3c, LR-001 §D) says published HMPE guidance is <=20% of break for multi-year life and that reaching it would require SF ~5, driving the mass ratio to order ~800x - and the repo files this as a 'named hard part' to be carried, not a gate to be passed. This assumption is weak: secondary-sourced, unspot-checked, and no planned experiment (ST-002 kitchen coupons at best) can produce multi-year creep data. The honest framing is that economics are being refined to the third decimal - and, on the branches, extended by thousands of lines of modal and ledger analysis - on a structural design point that may be off by a factor of six. A second, process-level hidden assumption surfaced by this review: that 'the repo is the single source of truth' (handoff.md:3,42) holds when the repo's remote carries 46 commits its own handoff does not know about. It does not; the assumption is only as good as the merge discipline behind it.

### Offers to other repos

- params/rev_a.json as the family's first cross-repo parameter file: Aster's K2 prototype on origin/claude/k2-momentum-bank already snapshots it with a sha256 (tools/k2-momentum-research/rev-a-snapshot.js, verified present) and the unmerged skyhook branch snapshots Aster's outputs back (pm001_aster_cells.json) - the two-way exchange exists, informally; a JSON schema plus the physics module as a tiny installable package would make it formal. Fix com_radius_km first.
- canon/registry.py + canon/audit.py (unmerged, claude/creative-canon-claim-audit): a repo-wide canon-claim registry and drift linter with a baseline ratchet and seeded-defect tests - directly reusable by Aster (DEC/OQ constants) and PartForge (grade thresholds) once merged and reviewed
- tests/test_tool_canon_snapshots.py pattern (unmerged base branch): HTML tools carry a JSON canon snapshot block, and a test fails if any value drifts from the canonical file - the cheapest fix for the 'simulators drift from canon' class across all family repos
- finance/export_grid.py's provenance envelope (schema_version, git_commit, source md5s canonicalised across line endings, caveats carried verbatim and asserted by tests, interpolation fidelity measured at export) - a Python-side sibling of Aster's EvidenceEnvelope and PartForge's headless JSON envelope
- The reconciliation-gate pattern (rightsize_sensitivity.py, demand_sensitivity.py): no derived analysis reports until it reproduces the tested anchors
- The Python->JS golden-run cross-verification (scripts/generate_golden.py + tools/twin/verify.mjs): a template for Aster's Lambert/propagator ports or PartForge geometry
- docs/DECISION_LOG.md + docs/DEVIATIONS.md as a two-file pattern: append-only record of how numbers got here, plus a register of every known non-canonical number with disposition and EXECUTED/OVERRIDDEN stamps (the branch's #4 'OVERRIDDEN 2026-10-22' row shows the pattern surviving a reversal)
- docs/verification_memo.md format (claim / verdict / finding & source with CONFIRMED / CORRECTED / NUANCE) - the family's cheapest red-team ritual
- tools/twin/ASSETS.md: provenance-for-art (source URL, retrieval date, license basis per texture) - adoptable verbatim by Idea-Bank and PartForge
- docs/outreach_log.md's framing 'commits don't count here, only signatures, quotes, and responses' as the family's external-progress metric
- ST-001 field kit (tools/sims/skyhook_st001_field_kit.html) and its capstan-grip rig: printable fixtures PartForge's Flight grade could declare and measure; the resulting splice data would be the family's first physical dataset

This is the only Phase 0 exit criterion a freshman can start alone, and it answers handoff.md:31 - the briefing says he is an NC State student, so the gate is already open.

### Time to v1

Two answers because the repo has two 'dones'. (a) Repo-level v1 = 'self-consistent Phase 0 paper study, remote reconciled with main, with its first proprietary datum' (the three moves above plus a creep spot-check): roughly 6-10 weekend sessions of 4-6 hours, fewer than the reviewer's 8-12 because the branches already contain the DEVIATIONS #4/#8 and pytest fixes and the leg make-or-buy model. At one session every two weeks during term that lands around February-March 2027; with winter-break work, late January 2027. [Likely] The reviewer's governing assumption - 'whether Hudson resumes at all' - is resolved: the remote shows Hudson directing agent sessions on 2026-10-22/23 (DEVIATIONS #4 row on the branch quotes his instruction), so the binding variable is now review-and-merge bandwidth, not resumption. (b) The repo's OWN definition of done, Phase 0 exit (validated multibody twin against flight data, joint statistics, capture demo video, regulatory engagement): not reachable solo on weekends at any date. It needs a lab partner (the NC State textiles path for joint statistics), tens of thousands of dollars for coupon-scale creep and splice testing, and years for the twin and grapple work; a credible partial Phase 0 (joints + a creep curve + a published gap-filling dataset) is a 2027-2028 outcome with an academic sponsor, and the full exit is a funded-team outcome with no honest date from here. [Speculative]

### Review deviation notes

No repo rule was deviated from: running `pytest tests/ -v` is skyhook's own Rule 1/9 (CLAUDE.md; README.md:32), and the briefing authorised test runs regardless. The Aster repo's AGENTS.md role reservation did not apply because I ran nothing in /home/user/asteroid-mining-planner (only git branch -r, git ls-tree, sed and grep). I installed nothing: pytest 9.1.1, openpyxl 3.1.5, numpy 2.5.3 and scipy 1.18.1 were already present (the earlier reviewer installed pytest). Commands that read tracked files but wrote nothing: pytest, python3 physics/skyhook_physics.py, python3 finance/skyhook_finance.py (opens the xlsx read_only=True), node tools/twin/verify.mjs, python3 -I re-derivations. I deliberately did NOT run finance/export_grid.py, finance/inspace_fork.py, finance/nre_decomposition.py, finance/reboost_bound.py or scripts/generate_golden.py because each writes a tracked file; tests/test_export_grid.py::test_committed_grid_is_not_stale rebuilds the grid in memory and passed. To measure the six unmerged remote branches without touching the clone I used read-only GitHub API GETs through gh (repo, branches, compare, pulls, actions/runs; the /pages and /branches/main/protection endpoints returned 403 from the proxy/integration), downloaded each branch tarball into /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/c0a4c4c5-ce12-5e4b-b0ad-1126d17baa10/scratchpad/skyhook-verify/tar_*/ and extracted to src_*/ there, and ran pytest in those copies with a per-directory `git` shim on PATH that answers `rev-parse HEAD` with the branch tip (the tarballs are not git repos and finance/export_grid.py:255 / tests/test_leg_make_or_buy.py shell out to git); without the shim each branch showed exactly one failure/error from that call, an environment artifact, not a code defect. No git fetch, checkout, add, commit, push, reset, stash or clean was run anywhere, and ASTER_PUSH_OK / ASTER_PROTECTED_OK were never set. Final `git status --short` per repo: skyhook clean (ignored .pytest_cache/, finance/__pycache__/, physics/__pycache__/, tests/__pycache__/ only); suborbital-leg-cost-model, Partforge, Idea-Bank clean; asteroid-mining-planner shows ' M .dispatch-scope' - its mtime is 2026-10-26 04:33:54 UTC and `git diff` shows it rewritten to '# aster-family — family hub scaffold (Hudson request, 2026-10-26 session)\naster-family/*' by another session before my first command (my scratchpad directory was created 05:31 UTC); I did not touch or revert it and record it here as instructed. I also read /root/.claude/skills/synced/<id>/rotovator-rev-a/SKILL.md read-only because handoff.md:6 names it as a drift vector.

## 2.3 PartForge


**Scope line (verbatim from the verified card):** hudsonclavin-cloud/Partforge (local: /home/user/Partforge, HEAD 0974a62 on main; single-branch shallow clone showing 58 commits, cut at 20027a2 'Gen 20.3' per .git/shallow; GitHub reports 94 commits on main, 7 branches, repo created 2026-08-26)

**One line.** A single-file browser CAD-from-prompt tool with a genuinely rigorous 'flight grade' self-check layer for rocket-club machined parts — every count I could re-run matches (733 + 20 + 27 Node checks, 21/21 headless parity in 238 s) — but it is 90% agent-authored (85 of 94 commits), has no CI, had one silent failed deploy, ships two pinned high-severity dependencies, and has never been proven on a single machined part.

**What it is.** PartForge is one 8,554-line, 1.24 MB index.html (README.md:12 'No backend. No build step. One HTML file.') that turns a text prompt or photo into parametric OpenSCAD via a bring-your-own-key LLM (15 OpenAI-compatible endpoints at index.html:1134 plus Anthropic at :1158), compiles it with openscad-wasm 0.0.4 pulled from jsdelivr (index.html:459) in a Web Worker, previews it in three.js (importmap :446-449) and exports STL/3MF. Its distinctive half is 'Flight grade': the generated file must carry a FLIGHT declaration (parseFlight :4672) naming material, process and every dimension a shop would measure with a tolerance and a provenance tag (cited/user/default); the program then measures the finished mesh with a ray-cast CMM (buildMeshIndex :3485, rayHits :3548, meshOverlap :3663, measureBore :3915), recomputes declared engineering checks (massProperties :566, isa :663, hoopStress :696, flutterVelocity :740, threadCheck :848, boltShear :881), assigns tier A/B/C (partTier :4771), marks default-provenance rows COMPUTED-not-PASS (:5820) and emits a manufacturing sheet or ICD. Fourteen reference tables (data/tables/*.json, 2.76 MB: 1037 certified motors, 299 O-rings, 282 drills, 291 airframe rows, 192 stock sizes, …) are projected into the page by tools/db/embed.mjs with per-row confidence labels and a row-by-row drift test. A headless/ Node package extracts 201 of the script's 441 top-level statements unedited (headless/core.gen.mjs:3, via acorn in build.mjs) into a CLI and a six-tool MCP server (stdio + Streamable HTTP), with a VM deploy kit; engine-study.html is an inert 'concept, not a plan' page (engine-study.html:204-210). Intended user: a university high-power rocket club (README.md:576-580). Recorded users so far: the author's agent sessions and nine Gemini runs (docs/runs/).

**Definition of done (the repo's own).** [Certain] The repo never defines 'v1' or 'done'. docs/STATUS.md:1 is 'Status — a stopping point': a claim/proof/re-run table (:7-34) plus per-Gen history. The nearest thing to a target is docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:240-257 'The five to build first': #1 LICENSE (done — LICENSE + NOTICE present, Apache-2.0), #2 data-integrity sweep ~4 d (partly done: GRADE_SY has grade 2 at index.html:5804; recall rows may not be cited :5544), #3 loads plumbing foundation ~5 d (:248-249 'Nothing else on this list should be built before this'), #4 aeroheating + opening shock ~8 d (:251-253), #5 tolerance stack-up 8 d (:255-257). grep of index.html: aeroheat 0, opening_shock 0, shock_ref 0, drawingDoc 0, informational 1 (inside a data literal at :4887) → #3-#5 unbuilt. The roadmap warns (:14) 'Nothing here was measured. These are estimates.' The implied v1 from README.md:299/:667 ('Machine to the sheet, not to the mesh') and :576-580 is: a club can hand a PartForge sheet to a shop and trust its numbers — which needs the as-built inspection entry the roadmap calls 'the missing half of the whole CMM story' (docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:266).

**Completion: 55% [Speculative].** Against the implied v1 (a club-usable shop-readiness tool validated on real hardware). Software: everything the README describes exists and every re-runnable claim passes (733 + 20 + 27 checks; 21/21 parity, 238 s; drift guard green) — but measured against the repo's own near-term list, 1 of 5 'build first' items is done and #3-#5 are unbuilt, so I score software 0.80 rather than 0.88. Validation: zero machined parts (docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:266); token costs 'Estimated, not measured' (docs/dry-run.md:24); live-model evidence is six Gemini rings at 0/6 first attempt, 6/6 by attempt 2, then one post-fix first-attempt pass before quota blocked the follow-up (docs/runs/2026-10-06-gemini-flight-x5/README.md:19-20, :49, :70); the shipped centering-ring template is 100% tool-default provenance (tests/templates/centering-ring.scad:16-19) and the CLI's human line says 'PASS … tier A' without saying so — 0.15. Hygiene: no CI; a Pages deploy failed on 2026-10-05 (run 37366327864) leaving the live site ~8.5 h behind main, unrecorded; 6 npm audit findings in headless prod deps (2 direct high); no tags/releases; stale counts one Gen after 'Counts corrected' (docs/STATUS.md:224 vs :310); drift guard skipped without npm install (tests/run.mjs:59-63) — 0.45. 0.6×0.80 + 0.3×0.15 + 0.1×0.45 ≈ 0.57 → 55. [Speculative] because the denominator and the weights are mine, not the repo's.

### Strengths

- [Certain] Measured, not asserted: 733 + 20 + 27 checks pass; 21/21 parity in 238 s; the headless core is bound to the browser code by an acorn drift guard (headless/build.mjs) so there is one implementation of the gate. The docs/STATUS.md:7-34 claim/proof/re-run table is the best 'what is verified' format in the five-repo family.
- [Certain] Refusal over invention is code: a missing bolt grade throws with the 1.6× reason (index.html:5806-5808); a unit-less DXF asks (55 assertions); an unknown motor says so (9 assertions); a recall row may not be cited (:5544); the CLI envelope's `assumptions` names how many inputs are defaults.
- [Certain] The provenance axis (cited/user/default) and COMPUTED-not-PASS (:5820) make 'who stands behind this number' visible without changing pass/fail — a good idea for a student shop.
- [Certain] Honest data layer: per-row certain/likely/recall and a disputed flag; docs/data/PROVENANCE.md:15 states that no standard was read from the standards body.
- [Certain] Above-average security posture for a BYOK static app: Host/Origin guard with reproduced attacks (server.mjs:38-58, tests/local-server.test.mjs); QR drawn locally; keyless endpoints send no Authorization header (headless test); timingSafeEqual token compare (headless/mcp-http.mjs:52); install.sh uses signed apt keyrings rather than curl|bash (deploy/install.sh:45-52); secret scan of tracked files 0 hits; my innerHTML audit found every model-text sink escaped or identifier-constrained (parseManifest index.html:5926-5934).
- [Certain] docs/roadmap-evaluation.md is a serious engineering critique that already changed shipping code (grade-2 fallback at :5804; inch clearances; QR leak).
- [Certain] The engine-study page is scoped as not-a-plan and tested to stay that way (engine-study.html:204-210; 9 tripwire tests, 40 planted violations caught per docs/STATUS.md:33).
- [Likely] Live-model evidence is recorded with the files (docs/runs/), including the finding that PartForge's own doctrine caused 5 of 6 first-attempt failures (README.md:20) and the fix commit (98df2a2).

### Weaknesses

- [Certain] One 8,554-line, 1.24 MB HTML file with seven 42-98 KB lines (:4880-4888) and module boundaries that exist only as /* X-BEGIN */ comment markers (tests/run.mjs:13-17). Maintainable by an agent with the whole file in context; hard for one freshman on a weekend.
- [Certain] 85 of 94 commits are authored 'Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>'; Hudson's 9 are two raw uploads and a rename (2026-08-26/30) and the six Codex PR commits/merges (2026-09-28..30). Nothing in README.md or docs says so, and the 2,490-line seed file's origin is unrecorded.
- [Certain] Zero external evidence of use: 0 stars, 0 forks, 0 issues, 0 releases; no club member, machined part or issue report in the record.
- [Certain] No CI; documented practice is push every commit to main (docs/STATUS.md:317), which Pages serves within a minute (README.md:873). One deploy already failed unnoticed (2026-10-05, 142b5ed) and a clean-looking merge once shipped a SyntaxError (docs/STATUS.md:118-120 context).
- [Certain] Reference tables 'compiled from memory of a standard … No standard was read from the standards body' (docs/data/PROVENANCE.md:15). Honestly labelled, but the seal and fastener data for a 60 bar end cap rests on transcriptions.
- [Certain] Two pinned direct dependencies with HIGH advisories in the hosted MCP surface (@modelcontextprotocol/sdk 1.31.0, zod 4.6.5 — headless/package.json:27-29; npm audit) plus a HIGH transitive body-parser; the VM kit installs them as-is.
- [Certain] A 994 KB generated file (headless/core.gen.mjs) is committed; every index.html change produces a ~1 MB diff, and the only guard against staleness is skipped unless headless/ is npm-installed (tests/run.mjs:59-63).
- [Certain] Documentation sprawl and rot: 60 KB README + 240 KB roadmap + 27 KB STATUS across 133 files; stale counts crept in within the Gen that claimed to fix them (docs/STATUS.md:224 vs :310); header stale (:3); headless/README.md:14-15 stale.
- [Likely] Effort allocation: Gen 37 spent three commits and ~50 tests on a page whose purpose is to do nothing (engine-study.html) while roadmap #3 — which the repo's own evaluation says must come first — is unbuilt.
- [Certain] 'No backend. No build step. One HTML file.' (README.md:12) is true of the browser app but the project has two build steps (tools/db/embed.mjs for data; headless/build.mjs for the core) and three runtime CDN dependencies (three.js, 14 MB openscad-wasm, qrcode-generator); the design loop depends on free-tier LLM quotas that blocked the last batch (docs/runs/2026-10-06-gemini-flight-x5/README.md:60-71).
- [Likely] openscad-wasm is GPL-2.0 (node_modules/openscad-wasm/package.json); headless/README.md:131-133 flags the hosted-build question without answering it.
- [Certain] API keys live in localStorage by design (index.html:464-465; README.md:307-308). My audit found no unescaped model-text innerHTML sink, but escapeHtml (:7087) escapes only & and <, so any future attribute-context use would be unsafe.

### Risks

| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Label |
|---|---|---|---|
| A wrong number reaches a machined part. The tool proves the mesh matches its own declaration; the shipped templates are 100% tool-default tolerances (tests/templates/centering-ring.scad:16-19) and the CLI's human line prints 'PASS … tier A → manufacturing sheet' with no provenance caveat; recall-grade gland data (docs/STATUS.md:274-278) on a clean sheet invites a student to build to it. The as-built check that would catch it does not exist (docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:266). | Medium [Likely] — 'Machine to the sheet, not to the mesh' (README.md:299) is doctrine, but students skim | High — physical: a coupler that jams at apogee or a seal that extrudes at 60 bar | [Likely] |
| Continuity. 85 of 94 commits are agent-authored at a cadence (46 commits in September, 12 in October on three days) the author cannot sustain by hand; if agent sessions stop, a 1.24 MB single file with comment-marker modules, a 994 KB generated twin and no CI becomes a frozen artifact. | High [Likely] — October already shows the drop (Oct 5-6, Oct 24-25 only) | High for the portfolio story; medium for the tool (it still runs) | [Likely] |
| Push-on-every-commit to a Pages-served main with no CI means one bad commit is live in a minute, and a failed deploy is silent. Already happened: run 37366327864 (2026-10-05, 142b5ed) failed and the site served the previous commit for ~8.5 h with nothing recorded; a merge once shipped a SyntaxError (docs/STATUS.md:118-120). | Medium [Certain] — observed twice | Medium — the live app fails to load or lags main until the next push | [Certain] |
| Hosted MCP surface runs on dependencies with open HIGH advisories (@modelcontextprotocol/sdk <=1.31.0 — session-binding and HTTP client issues per npm audit; zod <4.6.6 memory exhaustion; body-parser DoS). deploy/install.sh installs them unchanged on the VM. | Medium [Likely] — exact pins mean nothing updates without a deliberate bump | Medium — DoS or memory exhaustion on a hosted token-protected service; no key exposure identified | [Likely] |
| Reference data provenance: 'No standard was read from the standards body' (docs/data/PROVENANCE.md:15). A systematic transcription error shared by the three skeptic passes passes every test in the repo, because the embed test checks the projection against the JSON, not the JSON against the world. | Medium [Speculative] | High for seal and fastener rows; low for drills/pitches fixed by definition | [Speculative] |
| Provider churn: 15 endpoints, hard-coded model ids (claude-sonnet-5 at index.html:1161; gemini-3.6-flash), free tiers that log prompts (README.md:421-424). Each provider change costs a Gen. | High [Certain] — already seen: gemini-2.5-flash retired (commit 8e5315b), GPT-5 field rename | Low-medium — the keyless template path keeps working | [Certain] |
| GPL-2.0 engine in a hosted MCP service (headless/README.md:131-133; node_modules/openscad-wasm/package.json). Installed as a dependency, loaded from a CDN in the browser — probably compliant, but unresolved in writing. | Low [Speculative] | Low-medium (licensing cleanup, not a shutdown) | [Speculative] |
| Keys in localStorage + 21 innerHTML writes. Audited: model text is escaped or regex-constrained (index.html:5926-5934, :6583-6585, setOverlay callers); escapeHtml (:7087) is text-context only. A future attribute-context use would exfiltrate a key. | Low [Likely] | Medium — the user's own API credit, not others' | [Likely] |

### Hidden assumption

[Likely] The project rests on the assumption that measuring a mesh against the declaration the same model wrote — plus honest provenance labels — is meaningful evidence for a shop. The repo knows this is the weak point (docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:266 'every measurement in the app today is of the mesh'; README.md:299; index.html:5820), and today's shipped templates show how thin the labels are in practice: every tolerance on the centering ring is `tol_src:"default"` and the CLI still prints an unqualified 'PASS … tier A'. It stays weak until one real part is machined, measured with calipers and fed back in. The second, quieter assumption is that a university club will adopt a tool none of its members has touched; nothing in the record (0 issues, 0 external contributors, 0 releases) tests it.

### Offers to other repos

- The claim / proof / how-to-re-run table (docs/STATUS.md:7-34) as the family's standard 'what is verified' section (aster-family/CONVENTIONS.md:18 already names it).
- The single-file-app extraction pattern: headless/build.mjs (acorn parse → reference closure from 64 ROOTS → emit declarations unedited → `--check` drift guard; 181 lines). Applicable to Skyhook's tools/sims/*.jsx and *.html twins and Idea-Bank's HTML ideas so their math can be unit-tested in Node. Caveat to carry over: make the drift check unconditional, not skipped when deps are missing (tests/run.mjs:59-63).
- The result envelope {ok, tool, value, units, confidence, provenance, assumptions, validity_envelope, refusal{code,reason,what_would_help}, summary} (headless/partforge.mjs:4-5, :40-45) — near-identical to Aster's EvidenceEnvelope; one family JSON contract with a shared test vector is within reach.
- The MCP hosting kit: deploy/install.sh + partforge-mcp.service + Caddyfile.template (signed apt keyrings, locked user, token in header or path, Host allowlist, per-client rate limit, systemd sandbox) — reusable for hosting aster-mission-mcp; docs/MCP-STUDY.md §5 has cost numbers. Bump the two pinned deps first.
- Reference-table discipline: one JSON per table with per-row confidence/source/disputed, a build pipeline (tools/db/*.mjs), an embed step and a row-by-row embed-drift test (tests/flight-db-embed.test.mjs, 104 assertions) — a model for Skyhook's params/rev_a.json → simulator constants.
- The scope-tripwire test pattern (tests/engine-study.test.mjs + tests/engine-study-scope.mjs: number allowlist, forbidden vocabulary) — mechanical enforcement of Idea-Bank's 'Honest assessment' rule or Skyhook's 'label every claim' rule.
- Concrete link for Idea-Bank: 11 STL files across 7 idea directories plus tools/printcheck.py; `node headless/cli.mjs check <file.scad>` or `render_part` over MCP is a tested printability/measurement authority they could call.
- Concrete link for Skyhook: the ST-001 splice field kit (tools/sims/skyhook_st001_field_kit.html; handoff.md:15) needs physical fixtures; PartForge flight grade can declare, measure and sheet a printed or machined fixture. [Speculative: worth one]
- The local-proxy Host/Origin guard with reproduced attacks (server.mjs:38-58; tests/local-server.test.mjs) for any family repo that proxies a paid API key.

### Next three moves

1. 1. Hygiene before features (≈ half a weekend, agent-driven): add .github/workflows/ci.yml running `node tests/run.mjs` and `cd headless && npm ci && npm test` on push so the drift guard is never skipped; `npm audit fix`/bump @modelcontextprotocol/sdk and zod and rebuild; fix the stale lines (docs/STATUS.md:3, :84-85, :222-224; headless/README.md:14-15; docs/MCP-STUDY.md:48) to 201 of 441 statements, 64 roots, 733 + 20 + 27, 994 KB; date or delete README.md:414 'verified'; delete the six merged remote branches; tag the deployed commit (v6 → a git tag) and record the 2026-10-05 failed deploy; state in README that the code is agent-authored under Hudson's direction and where the 2026-08-26 seed file came from.
2. 2. Cut one real part (calendar-bound, not effort-bound): machine tests/templates/centering-ring.scad or docs/runs/2026-10-06-gemini-flight-x5/ring6-after-doctrine-fix.scad at the club mill, measure it with calipers, and build the as-built inspection entry (docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:266) so a COMPUTED row can become PASS from a measurement of metal; make the CLI's human line and the sheet say 'N of M tolerances are tool defaults' (the JSON already knows). Record it as docs/runs/<date>-first-article/ with photos and the diff. This changes the repo's category from self-consistent to validated.
3. 3. Build roadmap #3 before any new check (docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:248-249, ~5 engineer-days): non-gating row state, the ok-guard fix, the measured-input cross-reference from a loads row to the CMM rows; in the same pass, from a machine with egress, read Parker ORD 5700 §3.1.4 Figure 3-2 and Table 4-2 and close docs/STATUS.md:274-278. Only then #4/#5 or the release-package export (docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:270, ~1 d).

### Time to v1

[Speculative] To a defensible v1 (CI green, deps bumped, stale docs fixed, roadmap #3 built, Parker read, one machined part measured and fed back): the agent-driven software work is ~8-11 engineer-days by the roadmap's own estimates, which it warns are under-priced (docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:14), so call it 15-20 agent-days. At the September cadence (46 commits, ~10 active days) that is 6-10 weekend days ≈ 2-3 months of weekends — roughly January-March 2027 if Hudson spends most weekends on PartForge rather than Aster/Skyhook. The gating item is not effort: it is shop access at NC State to cut and measure one real part, which is semester-calendar-bound and has not happened in the 61 days of history (2026-08-26 → 2026-10-25). Without it the repo stays 'feature-complete, unvalidated' regardless of code work. Assumptions: freshman course load; October cadence already down to 12 commits on three days; agent sessions remain available; Gemini/other free tiers keep blocking batch runs (docs/runs/2026-10-06-gemini-flight-x5/README.md:60-71).

- README.md:12 'No backend. No build step. One HTML file.' — true of the browser app; the project nonetheless has two build steps (tools/db/embed.mjs, headless/build.mjs) and three runtime CDN dependencies. Framing, not falsehood.
- Not rot, verified correct: README.md:363 '36 machines'; README.md:686-690 data counts; README.md:103 default model claude-sonnet-5 (index.html:1161); 'six read-only tools'; parity timings within hardware variance; LICENSE/NOTICE present.
- Omissions a skeptical reader will find in ten seconds: nothing says 85 of 94 commits are agent-authored; nothing says where the 2,490-line seed file uploaded on 2026-08-26 came from; nothing records the 2026-10-05 failed Pages deploy; nothing records the npm audit findings.

### Review deviation notes

Read-only verification; no tracked file was created, edited, moved or deleted and no git write command was run (`git status --short` empty after every step). Partforge has no AGENTS.md or rules file reserving test-running for another role, so running its suites is not a role deviation; the task authorised it in any case. Commands with side effects, all in gitignored or untracked paths: `node tests/run.mjs` rewrote tests/.build/ (ignored by tests/.gitignore); `npm run test:parity` and two `node cli.mjs check` calls wrote the headless on-disk result cache (default PARTFORGE_CACHE location, untracked); `npm audit --omit=dev` read package metadata from the registry through the sandbox proxy and wrote nothing. headless/node_modules/ (93 packages) was already present from the prior reviewer's `npm ci`; I did not reinstall. No Playwright/Chromium is available, so none of tests/harness/*.mjs ran and the browser-harness rows of docs/STATUS.md remain unverified here; the live Pages URL is unreachable from the sandbox (CONNECT 403). GitHub REST reads via `gh api` succeeded. Scratch logs: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/c0a4c4c5-ce12-5e4b-b0ad-1126d17baa10/scratchpad/partforge-verify/run.log and /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/c0a4c4c5-ce12-5e4b-b0ad-1126d17baa10/scratchpad/partforge-verify/parity.log.

### Adversarial verification of the first-pass card

Verdicts on the reviewer's load-bearing claims: ADJUSTED 5, CONFIRMED 18, REFUTED 1.

- **ADJUSTED** — completion_percent 60 [Likely]: software 0.6×0.88 + validation 0.3×0.15 + hygiene 0.1×0.7 → Direction right, label and hygiene weight too generous. The repo never defines done (docs/STATUS.md:1 'a stopping point'), so the denominator is the reviewer's own, which is [Speculative] not [Likely]. Hygiene at 0.7 ignores: a Pages deploy that failed on 2026-10-05 (GitHub Actions run 37366327864, head 142b5ed, conclusion 'failure', build job 'cancelled'; next success 98df2a2 at 2026-10-06T04:20Z, so the live site sat ~8.5 h behind main unrecorded); npm audit on headless/ prod deps = 6 vulnerabilities (3 high) incl. two pinned direct deps (@modelcontextprotocol/sdk 1.31.0, zod 4.6.5 — headless/package.json:27-29); no tags or releases (git tag → 0; /releases → 0); a 994 KB generated file committed (headless/core.gen.mjs) whose drift guard is SKIPPED by `node tests/run.mjs` when headless/node_modules is absent (tests/run.mjs:59-63). Against the roadmap's own 'five to build first' (docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:240-257) only #1 is done and #2 is partial. I put it at 55% [Speculative].
- **REFUTED** — docs/STATUS.md:317 'Every commit is pushed to both the working branch and main' — UNVERIFIABLE because 'git branch -a shows only main and origin/main; no working branch exists on the remote' → The clone is single-branch, so `git branch -a` is not evidence about the remote. `git ls-remote --heads origin` returns 7 heads: main, claude/engine-design-study-u4d0ob (0974a62 — identical to main, i.e. exactly the documented practice for Gen 37), claude/gifted-thompson-9kyd8j (21bde81), and four codex/find-ways-to-run-with-gpt-api* branches (a773d74, 441f694, 95f1517, ebb9005). The STATUS claim is CONFIRMED for the latest Gen; the reviewer's evidence was an artifact of the clone.
- **ADJUSTED** — in_progress_or_unmerged: 'Nothing unmerged: only main exists locally and on origin' → 'Nothing unmerged' is right: all six non-main remote tips are ancestors of HEAD (`git merge-base --is-ancestor` true for each). 'Only main exists on origin' is wrong: six stale, merged feature branches remain on origin (GitHub API /branches lists the same 7). Cosmetic, but a skeptical reader running `git branch -r` after a full clone will see six dead branches.
- **ADJUSTED** — 58 commits visible; 52 of 58 authored 'Claude', 6 by hudsonclavin-cloud; commits are Gen N iterations, Sep-Oct 2026 → True of the shallow clone (.git/shallow = 20027a2, Gen 20.3). GitHub API /commits?sha=main returns 94 commits: 85 'Claude', 9 'hudsonclavin-cloud'. History starts 2026-08-26 (repo created_at 2026-08-26T21:15:27Z) with Hudson's own 'Add files via upload' of a 2,490-line `index (1).html` and `Start to git 3d.html`, renamed to index.html on 2026-08-30 (404abb7); 'Gen 1' is 2026-09-01 (bad65a7). The seed file's origin is recorded nowhere in the repo. So: Aug-Oct, 90% agent-authored across the full history, and the first ~2,500 lines predate the Gen series.
- **ADJUSTED** — Risk: keys in localStorage + 21 innerHTML writes; vpStatus.innerHTML (:7069) and notes (:6585) 'deserve an audit' — Low [Speculative] → Audited. 21 innerHTML assignments confirmed. `notes` (index.html:6583-6585) wraps the model's NOTE text in escapeHtml. All 14 non-literal `setOverlay(true, …)` callers (:6154, :7117-7118, :7583-7865, :7985-7987, :8518) either escapeHtml the model text or interpolate names that parseManifest constrains to `[A-Za-z_]\w*` (index.html:5926, :5932) and `hinge|slider` (:5932); same for `label.innerHTML` at :6252. `$('modelList').innerHTML` at :8356 escapes and adds &quot;. No unescaped model-text sink found. Residual: escapeHtml (:7087) escapes only & and <, so it is unsafe in attribute context, but I found no such use. Likelihood Low [Likely], not [Speculative].
- **ADJUSTED** — Minor citation accuracy: README.md:877 default model; docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:19 '7387 lines'; data/README.md:40 'all fourteen'; index.html:4880-4888 '48-98 KB' → README.md:877 is blank; the default-model statement is README.md:103 (and :879). The '7387 lines' self-flag is docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:18 (and :786), not :19 (blank). 'all fourteen' is data/README.md:47, not :40. Long data lines: 4880 (96 KB), 4881 (43), 4883 (98), 4884 (42), 4885 (89), 4887 (48), 4888 (49) → 42-98 KB, seven lines not nine.

What the first reviewer missed:

- [Certain] The remote has 7 branches, not 1: `git ls-remote --heads origin` lists main plus claude/engine-design-study-u4d0ob (== main at 0974a62), claude/gifted-thompson-9kyd8j and four codex/find-ways-to-run-with-gpt-api* branches; all are ancestors of HEAD. The reviewer's `git branch -a` only saw the single-branch clone, which led to a wrong UNVERIFIABLE on docs/STATUS.md:317 (the practice is in fact visible on the remote) and a wrong 'only main exists on origin'.
- [Certain] The full history is 94 commits (GitHub API), not 58: 85 'Claude', 9 'hudsonclavin-cloud'. The project began 2026-08-26 (repo created_at) with Hudson uploading a 2,490-line `index (1).html` and `Start to git 3d.html`, renamed 2026-08-30; Gen 1 is 2026-09-01. The briefing's 'Sep-Oct 2026' and the card's '52 of 58' understate both the span and the agent share, and the seed file's origin is recorded nowhere.
- [Certain] A GitHub Pages deployment FAILED: run 37366327864 at 2026-10-05T19:53:42Z (head 142b5ed; build job 'cancelled', deploy 'skipped'; the run for 250687d one minute earlier was 'cancelled'). The next success was 98df2a2 at 2026-10-06T04:20:57Z, so the live site served 8e5315b for ~8.5 h while main was two commits ahead. No doc mentions it. This is the concrete, already-happened instance of the no-CI/push-to-main risk the card rated hypothetically.
- [Certain] `npm audit --omit=dev` in headless/: 6 vulnerabilities (3 high, 3 moderate), including two exact-pinned DIRECT dependencies with HIGH advisories — @modelcontextprotocol/sdk 1.31.0 (<=1.31.0) and zod 4.6.5 (<4.6.6) at headless/package.json:27-29 — plus body-parser (high, transitive). deploy/install.sh installs them unchanged on the VM. Not mentioned anywhere in the card or the repo.
- [Certain] headless/core.gen.mjs (993,795 B) is a committed generated artifact (acknowledged at docs/MCP-STUDY.md:48 as '951 KB … committed'); every index.html change yields a ~1 MB diff, and its only staleness guard is SKIPPED by `node tests/run.mjs` when headless/node_modules/openscad-wasm is absent (tests/run.mjs:59-63), so docs/STATUS.md:310's 'must be green before any commit' can be green with a stale core. Also a 654 KB binary fixture is tracked (tests/fixtures/nosecone-vk-profile.stl.gz); repo is ~6 MB.
- [Certain] The shipped flight templates are 100% tool-default provenance (tests/templates/centering-ring.scad:16-19 — every row `tol_src:"default"`), and `node cli.mjs check … --human` prints 'PASS — … tier A → manufacturing sheet' with no caveat; only the JSON `assumptions` says '7 tolerance/load input(s) are defaults, not cited'. This sharpens risk #1 from hypothetical to 'the default demo already does it'.
- [Certain] No tags and no releases (git tag → 0; /releases, /tags → 0); the deployed app is identifiable only by commit SHA or the hand-bumped APP_VERSION 'v6' (index.html:8487).
- [Certain] The XSS concern was overstated and unaudited: I traced all 21 innerHTML sinks. `notes` (:6583-6585) is escaped; every non-literal setOverlay caller escapes or interpolates parseManifest-constrained identifiers (`[A-Za-z_]\w*`, `hinge|slider` at index.html:5926-5934); `$('modelList')` at :8356 escapes. No unescaped model-text sink exists today; the residual is that escapeHtml (:7087) is text-context only.
- [Certain] Several of the card's citations are off by one or more lines: docs/STATUS.md:12→:11 (733 row), :22→:21 (parity), :23→:22 (six tools); README.md:877→:103 (default model); docs/roadmap-evaluation.md:19→:18 ('7387 lines'); data/README.md:40→:47 ('all fourteen'); index.html:4880-4888 are seven lines of 42-98 KB, not nine of 48-98 KB. Minor, but a verifier following the citations would stumble.
- [Certain] 'No backend. No build step. One HTML file.' (README.md:12) sits beside two real build steps (tools/db/embed.mjs; headless/build.mjs) and three runtime CDN dependencies (three.js :447, 14 MB openscad-wasm :459, qrcode-generator :8045); the card mentioned the CDN risk but not qrcode-generator or the build steps.

## 2.4 Idea-Bank


**Scope line (verbatim from the verified card):** hudsonclavin-cloud/Idea-Bank (/home/user/Idea-Bank), main @ 1e5b7c6 (2026-10-24 15:52 -0400); origin/main identical; working tree clean

**One line.** A 75-folder gallery of AI-agent side projects with a strong per-folder contract and a locally green gate (1,161 node tests of which 1,157-1,158 pass and 3 skip, 108 Python tests, 148/148 phone+desktop smoke checks, 11/11 STLs closed solids, 4/4 gallery checks), but no LICENSE, no CI, no pinned dependencies, no tags, a commit log whose review messages point at the wrong idea numbers, and one UI test that fails under CPU load.

**What it is.** Idea-Bank is a static GitHub Pages gallery (index.html, 75 cards, no JavaScript) where each `ideas/NN-slug/` folder is one self-chosen agent project: a browser explorable, tool, game, printable object, data essay, or art piece, written up with a README that must carry Kind/Run/Made by, What/Why/How, an Honest assessment with [Certain]/[Likely]/[Speculative] labels, and Where next (README.md:88-97 'House rules for a deposit'). Shipped pages run offline from file:// with no build step and no network (0 fetch/XHR/WebSocket, 0 external script or stylesheet tags, 0 type=module in shipped .js/.html; the only network code is 75's optional data/fetch.mjs rebuild script, README.md:91). Five tools (tools/check.mjs 142 lines, smoke.mjs 227, gallery.mjs 298, gallery-check.mjs 79, printcheck.py 214; 960 lines total) enforce README shape, folder size, per-idea tests, a headless Chromium smoke test at 1280 and 390 px, gallery image integrity, and STL printability. Six entries are orbital-mechanics or space-adjacent (03 CR3BP hilltops, 27 flyby frames, 41 analemma, 42 reaction wheels, 72 Kirkwood gaps from 1,576,490 JPL SBDB orbits, 73 Kepler-law gears) and seven ideas ship 11 STL files. It was built in three days (2026-10-22..24: 28, 71 and 9 commits) across 108 commits, 98 authored 'Claude' and 10 by Hudson (five PR merges, three bulk commits of Codex-attributed ideas 11-30 and 51-60, the initial commit, and one gallery-link fix). Its audience today is Hudson and anyone he shows the gallery to; it is a portfolio and a sandbox, not a product.

**Definition of done (the repo's own).** The repo never defines a repo-level v1. 'Done' is defined per deposit only: README.md:88-97 'House rules for a deposit' (runs with nothing installed :92; opens from the gallery :93; works on a phone at 390 px :94; proves itself with test_*.py / *.test.mjs plus the smoke test :95; says what it is with the fixed README sections and a labelled Honest assessment :96; stays under about 500 KB :97), enforced by `node tools/check.mjs` (tools/check.mjs:15-16 header fields and only three of the five sections, :49 Kind non-empty only, :52 sections demoted to warnings, :13-14 and :112-113 warn at 500 KB / fail at 650 KB, :61-91 tests and printcheck, :83 'no automated tests found' is a note not a failure, :88 printcheck 'skipped' counts as pass, :126-136 smoke and gallery-check). The only tracked forward plan is one sentence in PR #5's body: 'Wave 2 (76–80) has not been started.' There is no STATUS, roadmap, issue, milestone, tag or release (git tag: 0; releases API: 0; open_issues_count 0; issues?state=all returns only the 5 merged PRs).

**Completion: 90% [Likely].** Against its own per-deposit contract every suite the gate invokes is green when run uncontended: node --test 1,161 tests (1,157 pass, 1 fail, 3 skipped in my run, 371.7 s; the one failure, ideas/63-still-rising/ui.test.mjs:116 phone touch-drag, passed 4/4 when re-run alone, matching the reviewer's 1,158/0), Python 108/108, smoke 148/148 in 13.0 min, gallery-check 4/4, printcheck 11/11, and a six-idea check.mjs spot run (05, 08, 09, 21, 31, 73) all 'ok'. What remains is small but real: 2 ideas have no automated test (05-rimsong has only a manual verify.html; 09-unconformity has none; check.mjs:83 passes them with a note), 2 READMEs lack required sections (08 lacks 'How it works' and 'Where it could go next'; 31 lacks 'How it works'; check.mjs:16,52 checks three of five and only warns), 6 Kinds break the one-or-two-words rule (21, 23, 24, 25, 27, 30; check.mjs:49 unchecked), and 73-kepler-pair is 546 KB against the 500 KB rule (check.mjs:13-14 tolerates up to 650; 68, 75 and 09 sit at 492/470/458 KB). If Wave 2 (76–80) counts, 75/80 of the planned count exists. The 10 points withheld are for those contract gaps, the load-sensitive UI test (a CI blocker in waiting), and the repo-level items the family docs already flag (LICENSE, CI, a recorded review process with correct idea numbers), which the README does not list as rules but which a 'bank' needs before anyone else can withdraw from it.

### Strengths

- [Certain] The entire gate reproduces green when measured uncontended by a second, adversarial run: 1,161 node tests (1,157 pass under load with the one failure passing 4/4 alone; 3 skipped), 108 Python tests, 148/148 smoke checks in 13.0 min, 11/11 STLs, 4/4 gallery checks, with a clean working tree afterwards
- [Certain] The deposit contract plus 960 lines of tooling is a genuinely reusable mini-framework for keeping many unrelated small projects browsable, phone-safe and non-rotting; it is the only thing in the family that already tests at phone width (smoke.mjs:38-40, 168-174)
- [Certain] Confidence labels are used at scale and honestly: 385 [Certain] / 184 [Likely] / 110 [Speculative] across 75 READMEs, every README has at least one, and the assessments record what was wrong before review (03 README:78 resonance overclaim; 42 README:88 solar-pressure torque 5-10x too high; 72 README:59,119 tagline wrong at the 2:1; 70 README:56,102 lead fact reversed)
- [Certain] Provenance in the data ideas is machine-checked: 68 pins SHA-256s in data/SOURCE.md and re-verifies in check.mjs:35-41; 72's belt-data.js:1-15 header carries source, date, count and page hashes; 75 hash-pins PREREGISTRATION.md at data.test.mjs:119-120
- [Certain] 'Words tests' pin prose to code: 73 page.test.mjs:1,14-24 and 72 belt.test.mjs:514 assert that README/page figures equal what the code computes and that review corrections stay made. This is a direct answer to doc drift
- [Certain] Zero runtime network, zero external scripts, zero secrets, zero TODO/FIXME in shipped code; one `new Function` (38-hinge, compiled from a validated AST) is the only eval-like construct; localStorage limited to prefs/progress in 16 ideas; pages open from file:// (no type=module; gallery-check verifies file:// for the gallery itself)
- [Likely] The space-adjacent physics is real engineering, not decoration: 03 uses canonical momenta and a symplectic Yoshida-4 integrator with Jacobi-constant drift tests and records the 3:1 and 2:1 resonant mass ratios (README:57-58); 42 implements a Lie-Poisson splitting for a gyrostat and cross-checks against RK4; 73 derives deterministic sin/cos/atan2 so committed STLs are byte-identical across engines

### Weaknesses

- [Certain] No LICENSE: a public repo with none is all-rights-reserved by default, which contradicts the open posture and blocks the other family repos from lifting tools/smoke.mjs or printcheck.py cleanly
- [Certain] No CI, no pinned dependencies (no package.json/lockfile), and a 20-30 minute local gate; nothing runs on push, and the one load-sensitive Playwright test (63) would make a first CI run red
- [Certain] The verification story is AI-only, uneven, and now mislabelled in git: 37/75 ideas never had the independent review the PRs describe; reviewer identities and prompts are not tracked; 'Made by' personas (29 prefixed 'Codex /', 0 mentioning Claude) do not match git authorship (98 commits by 'Claude'); and 30 review commits carry pre-renumbering idea numbers that now point at the unreviewed cohorts
- [Certain] Depth varies by an order of magnitude between cohorts: Codex-attributed 27-slingshot-atelier is a 22-line minified model with a 9-line test; Claude-attributed 73-kepler-pair is ~3,900 lines of text with four test files. The gallery presents them as peers
- [Certain] Nothing physical or human has been verified: no STL printed, 67 never played by two people, Gait never played, Rimsong never heard, no real phone or non-Chromium browser (PR #1, #3, #4, #5 'Not verified' sections)
- [Certain] House rules drift from the tool: sections, Kind length, the 500 KB budget, 'gets a test' and the STL gate are stated as rules but enforced as warnings, notes or not at all; 73's README cites a '450 KB guide' that does not exist
- [Certain] No shared code or data layer: 71k lines of .js across 175 files (109k across 300 with .mjs/.cjs) with every idea self-contained, so a fix to a shared idiom (e.g. the UMD wrapper used by 03/27/42/73) is 75 edits
- [Likely] Repo weight is already 16 MB working tree / 9.2 MB .git after three days (5.3 MB of tracked images and STLs; largest file 182 KB); with 68, 75 and 09 already at 458-492 KB, Wave 2 printables will push folders past the hard 650 KB limit unless STLs are generated rather than committed

### Risks

| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Label |
|---|---|---|---|
| The 'honest assessment' brand is undermined when a reader discovers that half the ideas were never independently reviewed, that no reviewer is named anywhere in the repo, and that `git log` on main now shows 'Fix idea 15 after independent review' against folders that were never reviewed | High | Medium: reputational for the portfolio; low for code correctness since the tests themselves are strong | [Certain] on the facts (37/75 unreviewed per PR bodies; 30 renumbered review commits verified via git show --stat); [Likely] on the consequence |
| No LICENSE makes every artifact legally all-rights-reserved; a future collaborator or the Aster family cannot reuse the tools or physics modules without a written grant | Certain today | Medium, and a five-minute fix | [Certain] |
| Without CI and with unpinned Playwright/trimesh, the gate stops being run as the bank grows (20-30 min locally); the first CI attempt will also hit the load-sensitive 63 test and the vacuous-without-trimesh STL gate (check.mjs:88), so rot enters silently or CI gets ignored as 'flaky' | High | Medium: individual ideas break on Pages without anyone noticing | [Certain] on gate duration, the skip behaviour and the observed under-load failure; [Likely] on rot |
| The seven printables may not fit or print as described (0.48 mm tooth flanks in 73; 1 mm bars and 0.6 mm tick bumps in 72); a first physical print could invalidate several [Certain] geometry claims' practical meaning | Medium | Low-medium: each README already states this, so credibility survives, but the objects do not | [Certain] that none has been printed; [Speculative] on failure modes |
| Authorship ambiguity (persona names, 'Codex /' prefixes on 29 of 30 bulk-committed ideas but not 22, git author 'Claude', bulk commits by Hudson) complicates any later claim about who built what if the portfolio is used for admissions, hiring or the Aster trademark story | Medium | Medium | [Certain] on the mismatch |
| Bundled data snapshots (JPL 2026-10-23, IERS 2026-10-23, USGS 2008-2025) age; the re-verify paths require network and were not exercisable here | Low | Low: snapshots are dated and hashed, which is the right design | [Certain] |

### Hidden assumption

That an AI agent's self-written Honest assessment, checked by a second AI agent told to refute it, is an adequate substitute for human and physical verification. It holds well for claims that tests can pin (orbital-mechanics identities, mesh closure, data arithmetic), which is why the gate is green on re-run. It is weak exactly where the bank makes its most interesting claims: that printed objects fit and feel right (7 ideas, 0 printed), that two-player and audio pieces work for people (67, 01, 05 never tried by humans), and that pages work on a real phone (headless Chromium only, and one touch-drag test already fails under load). The repo never records who the reviewers were or what they were told, and its commit log now mislabels which ideas the review touched, so even the strong half of the assumption cannot be audited from the repo alone. [Certain] on the facts; [Likely] that this is the load-bearing assumption.
