- The other repos mention Idea-Bank only in asteroid-mining-planner/aster-family/{NAME_AND_TRADEMARK.md:62, CONVENTIONS.md:19-20 and 26-28, MANIFEST.json:138-163 'ideabank' member, site/index.html:119-126,143}; git grep in Partforge, skyhook and suborbital-leg-cost-model finds 0 files

### Offers to other repos

- tools/smoke.mjs (227 lines): a generic, Playwright-only headless gate at 1280 and 390 px that fails on console errors, failed local requests, missing viewport meta and horizontal overflow (smoke.mjs:144-174). Aster's Pages site and PartForge's single-file index.html have no phone-width gate today; this one drops in unchanged (it resolves pages relative to its own repo root, smoke.mjs:15,75, so copy it rather than import it)
- tools/printcheck.py (214 lines): STL watertightness, bed fit, overhang area, sampled wall thickness and optional PrusaSlicer slice. PartForge is named the family's printability authority (aster-family/site/index.html:143) yet 'no code link today'; this is the link, and it passes PartForge-style checks on all 11 STLs here in under 2 s
- The 'words test' pattern (ideas/73-kepler-pair/page.test.mjs:1-24; ideas/72-kirkwood-ruler/belt.test.mjs:514): tests that assert README/page figures equal computed values and that review corrections persist. Skyhook's handoff.md:1 is dated 31 Aug 2026 against a 2026-09-10 HEAD (verified); this pattern would catch that class of drift
- tools/gallery.mjs (298 lines): README-header-driven index and ledger generation between '<!-- gallery:start/end -->' markers with preview capture. A family landing page listing five repos is the same shape as 75 ideas; the generator could build aster-family/site from each repo's README header
- Pure physics modules with tests, UMD-wrapped for browser and Node: 03 physics.js (CR3BP, Lagrange points, Routh limit, symplectic integrator), 42 attitude.js (reaction-wheel gyrostat and desaturation), 73 kepler.js (Kepler's equation, true/mean anomaly, deterministic trig), 27 model.js (hyperbolic flyby turning angle). Aster's 'about' or education surfaces could host 03 and 27 as explorables next to the porkchop plots
- Provenance envelope shapes: 72 belt-data.js:1-15 header (source, queried date, count, fields, per-page SHA-256), 68 data/SOURCE.md (retrieval date, SHA-256, processing steps, verbatim excerpt) verified by 68/check.mjs:35-41, 75 PREREGISTRATION.md hash-pinned at data.test.mjs:119-120. These are the closest thing in the family to Aster's EvidenceEnvelope expressed as plain files, and the pre-registration pattern maps directly onto Skyhook's 'lock the conservative case before computing' discipline
- The deposit contract itself (README.md:88-97 'House rules'): title/tagline/Kind/Run/Made by + What/Why/How/Honest assessment/Where next, enforced by a script. A per-slice or per-finding README in Skyhook finance/ could adopt it verbatim; adopt the checker with the three gaps (sections, Kind length, size) closed first

Add LICENSE (MIT or Apache-2.0, '(c) 2026 Hudson Clavin'), a package.json that pins playwright (1.63.0 works here) with a lockfile and a `requirements.txt` for trimesh/scipy/networkx/rtree, and a one-job .github/workflows/check.yml that runs `node tools/check.mjs --no-smoke` on every push and `node tools/smoke.mjs` + `gallery-check.mjs` nightly (split because the full gate is 20-30 min). Install trimesh in the workflow so the STL gate is not vacuous, make check.mjs:88 treat 'skipped' as a failure in CI, and give ideas/63-still-rising/ui.test.mjs:95-101 a settling wait between touchMove events so the first CI run is green. About one afternoon
2. Write PROCESS.md from PR bodies #1/#3/#4/#5: the build -> hostile review -> fix -> read-only verify loop, the list of 38 reviewed and 37 unreviewed ideas (derivable by grepping READMEs for '## Revision note' plus 03 and 06), a mapping table from the 30 pre-renumbering commit subjects to today's idea numbers, and a rule that every 'Revision note' names the reviewing agent and date. In the same pass, tighten check.mjs: all five sections as errors (lines 16, 52), Kind limited to two words (line 49), size failure at 500 KB (line 112), untested ideas as a warning (line 83), and fix the two READMEs (08, 31), six Kinds, and 73's '450 KB' line. This turns the family's first [Speculative] physical claim into data, gives PartForge a real test article, and tells you whether Wave 2 (76-80) should be more printables or more explorables. ### Time to v1

Per the repo's own per-deposit contract it is already at v1 for 75/75 folders, so 'v1' here means a licensed, CI-gated, dependency-pinned, process-documented bank at the planned 80 ideas with correct review attribution. Moves 1 and 2 are one to two weekends of Hudson's time (mostly reading and small edits; the agents do the rest, and the 63 test fix is a few lines). Wave 2 (76-80) at the observed rate (75 ideas in three days of agent sessions, with review as the bottleneck) is one more weekend plus a review pass. Call it 2-3 weekends of weekend-only work, so mid-to-late November 2026 [Likely]. Physical validation of the printables is open-ended and depends on printer access, not calendar time [Speculative].

### Doc rot and false claims

- README.md:96 'Kind: (one or two words)' is violated by 6 ideas (21, 23, 24, 25, 27, 30) and unenforced (check.mjs:49)
- README.md:96 lists five required sections; 08 and 31 lack some; check.mjs:16 checks three and :52 only warns
- README.md:97 'Under about 500 KB per folder' vs check.mjs failing only at 650 KB (lines 13-14) and 73 at 546 KB; ideas/73-kepler-pair/README.md:125 cites a 'house 450 KB guide' that does not exist in the repo
- README.md:95 'Logic worth trusting gets a test' vs 05-rimsong and 09-unconformity having none; check.mjs:83 passes them with a note rather than a warning
- README.md:106 implies printcheck gates every STL idea; in practice check.mjs:88 counts 'skipped (pip install trimesh to enable)' as a pass, so on a machine without trimesh the STL gate is a no-op
- Git history misattributes review: 30 commits titled 'Fix idea NN after independent review' (NN 11-30) or 'Revise 5N … after independent review' (51-60) were made on the batch branches before renumbering (+20 and +10); on main those numbers belong to the Codex-made ideas PR #3 and PR #4 say were NOT reviewed (verified: 01b579c touched ideas/15-lies-flat, now 35; d807bee touched ideas/51-isomorphs, now 61)
- The review process ('independent hostile review', 'read-only verifier', 'at most one re-fix round') exists only in PR bodies; the repo's README never mentions review, and 'Made by' personas cannot be traced to a model or session
- asteroid-mining-planner/aster-family/site/index.html:124 says '97 test files'; the count is 98 (86 .test.mjs + 1 .test.cjs + 11 test_*.py)
- aster-family/MANIFEST.json:157 describes Idea-Bank's test_command as 'node tools/check.mjs (README format, size, per-idea tests, headless smoke)' without noting that it takes 20-30 min, needs a global Playwright, and skips the STL check without trimesh
- PR #4 body still refers to 'Main's own 51–60 were not reviewed here' and nothing later says they were; the gallery and README present 51-60 as peers of reviewed ideas
- The previous reviewer's card said 'zero eval in shipped code'; ideas/38-hinge/js/hinge.js:488 uses `new Function` (AST-compiled, with a closure fallback). It also said 17 ideas use localStorage (16 do), tools are ~1,100 lines (960), and 73 is 4,669 lines (3,908 text lines; 4,507 with binary STLs counted)
- The Actions history is not all green: the first 'pages build and deployment' run (83f9e03, 2026-10-22) failed in Jekyll; PR #2 (.nojekyll) fixed it. Harmless, but a reader of the Actions tab sees a red run

### Review deviation notes

Ran the repo's test suites for measurement, as authorized for this read-only review (Idea-Bank has no AGENTS.md reserving test-running, so no role rule was overridden). Installed nothing: trimesh 5.2.0, scipy, networkx and rtree were already importable in the user site from the previous reviewer's pip --user install; Playwright 1.63.0 and Chromium were preinstalled; `playwright install` was not run. The full node suite (87 files) was run in one background job followed by the full smoke sweep (74 dirs); because I ran a six-idea check.mjs subset, gallery-check and three 40-two-sheets checks in the foreground during the node run, one Playwright touch-drag test (ideas/63-still-rising/ui.test.mjs:116) failed under contention and then passed 4/4 when re-run alone; both results are reported above rather than hidden. Nothing modified any tracked file in Idea-Bank: `git status --short` is empty after every run; `git status --ignored` shows only ideas/*/__pycache__/ directories from unittest (ignored by .gitignore:2). Scratch output (driver.log, node-test.log, smoke.log, smoke-shots/) is under /tmp/claude-0/-home-user/c0a4c4c5-ce12-5e4b-b0ad-1126d17baa10/scratchpad/ideabank-verify/. The Aster repo shows ' M .dispatch-scope' (mtime 2026-10-26 04:33:54; diff replaces the S18 scope with '# aster-family — family hub scaffold (Hudson request, 2026-10-26 session)' / 'aster-family/*'); that predates my first command (~05:43Z) and I ran only git status/log/diff, sed and grep there. Partforge, skyhook and suborbital-leg-cost-model are clean. Live GitHub Pages content was not fetched (github.io and the Pages API endpoint are proxy-blocked); deployment facts come from the Actions API. The 68 IERS and 72 JPL re-verification paths need network and were not exercised.

### Adversarial verification of the first-pass card

Verdicts on the reviewer's load-bearing claims: ADJUSTED 7, CONFIRMED 17, UNVERIFIABLE 1.

## 2.5 suborbital-leg-cost-model


**Scope line (verbatim from the verified card):** suborbital-leg-cost-model (/home/user/suborbital-leg-cost-model; GitHub hudsonclavin-cloud/suborbital-leg-cost-model, private, not archived)

**One line.** A one-commit, zero-code scaffold whose entire mandate was folded back into skyhook ~21 hours after it was created; it should be archived with a pointer README, not revived.

**What it is.** This repo was meant to hold sourced suborbital-provider pricing and a procure-vs-develop trade model for the Skyhook rotovator's rocket leg (README.md:3-11), answering DECISION_LOG item 9's finding that the Phase 2 valley is an NRE problem, not a hardware problem. Today it contains seven files totaling 134 lines: README, CLAUDE.md, empty STATUS/INVARIANTS/DEVLOG scaffolding, a decision-doc template, and a .gitignore (git ls-files). There is no src/, no docs/providers/, no tests, no data, and no sourced price; README.md:13 says "bootstrapped 2026-07-07 — nothing works yet" and that is still exactly true [Certain]. The same day it was created, skyhook commit 538ffc7 (2026-07-07 21:35 -0400) absorbed its mandate into skyhook/docs/providers/README.md, and skyhook/finance/leg_cost.py (commits 0dd8f0a, ff24a23) now carries the develop-path leg-cost model. For Hudson, this repo is now a historical artifact and a potential trap for agents who read STATUS.md:7 "Suggested first: provider landscape scan" and start duplicating work that belongs in skyhook.

**Definition of done (the repo's own).** The repo never defines v1 or "done" explicitly [Certain]. The implicit mandate is in README.md:3-4 ("Sourced suborbital provider pricing research plus the procure-vs-develop trade model for the Skyhook program's launch segment") and README.md:9-11 (trade is NRE-down vs marginal-up; must be modeled with sourced provider pricing before any anchor conversation approaches terms). CLAUDE.md:17-19 adds a gate: any model extending skyhook/finance/ must first reproduce skyhook's tested anchors (pattern: rightsize_sensitivity.py). STATUS.md:7-11 names the first task (provider landscape scan at ~4-5 km/s class) and two parked items (trade-model skeleton blocked on at least one sourced price; reconciliation gate against the $87,986/catch anchor). CLAUDE.md:24,27-28 say src/ and docs/providers/ are created "when the first module lands" / "on first source" — neither exists. So the closest thing to a done-definition is: (1) at least one primary-sourced provider price in docs/providers/, (2) a trade model in src/ that passes the $87,986/catch reconciliation gate, (3) a procure-vs-develop finding.

**Completion: 0% [Certain].** Against its own implicit definition of done, nothing has been started: git ls-files shows 7 files, none of them code or data; `find` confirms no src/ and no docs/providers/; INVARIANTS.md sections 1-3 are all "(empty)" (lines 13-14, 18-19, 23); DEVLOG.md:11 "Nothing runs yet"; the only commit is the init (git log --all shows 9e6eef3 alone; GitHub REST reports 1 commit, 1 branch, pushed_at 2026-07-07T05:18:32Z). The scaffolding itself is complete and well-formed, but scaffolding is 0% of the mandate. Separately, the mandate's progress in skyhook is partial (develop-path model runs and reproduces its findings; procure-path sourced pricing still has no provider file and the model's procure anchor is unsourced), but that progress belongs to skyhook's card, not this one.

- Radically honest status: README.md:13, STATUS.md:6 and DEVLOG.md:11 all say nothing works, and that is exactly what the repo contains. No overclaiming anywhere. [Certain]
- The fold-back decision was fast and correct: within ~21 hours the mandate was moved to skyhook (commit 538ffc7) with a stated rationale — keep the model coupled to the tested finance/skyhook_finance.py anchors instead of mirroring them across repos (skyhook/docs/providers/README.md:12-14). That avoided a cross-repo number-drift problem before it started. [Likely]
- CLAUDE.md:14-21 'Inherited discipline' block is a tight, reusable pattern for a satellite repo that consumes a parent read-only: no unsourced numbers, reconciliation gate before findings, never edit upstream. [Certain]
- docs/decisions/TEMPLATE.md is a clean 26-line decision-doc skeleton (Intent / Inherited constraints / New invariants / OQs / DECs with DRAFT→PROPOSED→LOCKED / Phases / Out of scope / Engineering record) that matches the slice-discipline vocabulary used in asteroid-mining-planner. [Certain]
- Clean history: one commit, clean tree, local == remote, .gitignore in place. Nothing to untangle. [Certain]

### Weaknesses

- It is an attractive nuisance: an unarchived GitHub repo whose STATUS.md:7-8 says 'Suggested first: provider landscape scan' and whose CLAUDE.md:24 says 'create src/ when the first module lands'. Any agent or future-Hudson landing here will start work that already has a home in skyhook. [Certain]
- No forward pointer: nothing in this repo says it was superseded. The migration note exists only on the skyhook side (docs/providers/README.md:12-14). [Certain]
- Zero substantive content: 134 lines, all process scaffolding; the three 'operating system' files (STATUS/INVARIANTS/DEVLOG) are empty shells. [Certain]
- The one unique artifact (TEMPLATE.md) is an orphaned convention — no other repo in the family uses the DRAFT→PROPOSED→LOCKED decision-doc format (grep across /home/user found only this file), so it has had no effect. [Certain]
- The repo's reason for existing — sourced provider pricing — never produced a single sourced price, here or (in docs/providers/) in skyhook, 3.5 months on. [Certain]

### Risks

| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Label |
|---|---|---|---|
| Duplicate / divergent work: an agent follows STATUS.md:7 here and builds a second provider scan or trade model disconnected from skyhook's tested anchors — the exact drift the fold-back was meant to prevent. | Medium while the repo stays unarchived and pointer-less; drops to near zero once archived. | Medium: a wasted session and two sets of leg-cost numbers to reconcile. | [Likely] |
| Provenance rot if deleted rather than archived: skyhook/docs/providers/README.md:12 cites this repo by name as the migration source; deletion makes that citation dangle and loses the single commit that documents the original mandate wording. | Low (only if Hudson chooses delete). | Low: the mandate text is duplicated in skyhook, so only the audit trail is lost. | [Certain] |
| The procure-vs-develop decision gets made on an unsourced procure price: leg_cost.py:29 hardcodes $15M 'Alpha' with no source comment, while DECISION_LOG item 9 forbids approaching anchor terms without sourced provider pricing and outreach to Rocket Lab is already underway (skyhook/docs/outreach_log.md:10). | Medium-High: docs/providers/ has been empty since 2026-07-07. | High for the Skyhook program: the pivotal business-architecture decision rests on a placeholder. | [Likely] |
| Documentation claims a reconciliation-gated trade model (skyhook/docs/providers/README.md:9-10,23-25) that does not exist; an investor-facing audit trail ('this repo's audit trail is investor-facing', line 21-22) that promises a gate it never implemented. | Certain that the gap exists today. | Medium: credibility, not safety. | [Certain] |

### Hidden assumption

The whole repo (and its successor in skyhook) assumes a suborbital leg at the ~4.6 km/s ground-relative catch velocity (skyhook/docs/providers/README.md:30-31; leg_cost.py output 'v_catch_ground_rel=4.591 km/s') can be PROCURED from a commercial provider at a knowable price, so that an NRE-down vs marginal-up trade is even well-posed. The evidence so far says that assumption is weak: skyhook's own MP-001 (docs/research/mp001_market_pricing.md:18) finds HASTE is 'contract-implied $9.5M/flight — no $/kg possible: payload-at-trajectory undisclosed', and the model's only procure anchor is an unsourced $15M figure for Alpha, an orbital launcher, not a suborbital rendezvous service. If no one sells this leg, the 'procure' branch collapses and the trade reverts to develop-path NRE, which LEG_COST_FINDINGS.md shows does not clear the $2,835/kg ask at Conservative inputs for any payload. [Likely weak]

### Depends on other repos

- skyhook — this repo exists only to serve it: motivation from skyhook/finance/RIGHTSIZE_FINDINGS.md and docs/DECISION_LOG.md item 9 (README.md:4-11); reconciliation gate against skyhook/finance/rightsize_sensitivity.py and skyhook_finance.py (CLAUDE.md:17-19, STATUS.md:9-11); canonical parameters from skyhook/params/rev_a.json (skyhook/docs/providers/README.md:30-31). The repo is read-only toward skyhook by rule (CLAUDE.md:20-21).

### Offers to other repos

- docs/decisions/TEMPLATE.md — a compact 8-section decision-doc template (Intent, Inherited constraints, New invariants, OQs, DECs with DRAFT→PROPOSED→LOCKED, Phases, Out of scope, Engineering record). skyhook has no docs/decisions/ directory and no equivalent template; an Aster family-conventions location could adopt it as the standard decision record. [Certain it is unique; Speculative on fit]
- The 'full tier' bootstrap kit as a family repo template: README with repo-map table + CLAUDE.md + STATUS/INVARIANTS/DEVLOG + decisions/TEMPLATE + .gitignore (README.md:15-23, DEVLOG.md:7-8 call this 'full tier'). asteroid-mining-planner already runs populated STATUS/INVARIANTS/DEVLOG files, so the shapes are compatible. [Likely]
- CLAUDE.md:14-21 'Inherited discipline' block — the pattern for any satellite repo that consumes a parent repo: no unsourced numbers, reconciliation gate before findings, read-only upstream (import/copy with citation). Directly applicable to a Partforge-serves-skyhook or Idea-Bank-feeds-Aster relationship. [Likely]
- A worked example of the right call: fold a satellite back into its parent when the satellite would have to mirror tested anchors (skyhook/docs/providers/README.md:12-14). Useful precedent when deciding whether new Aster family ideas deserve their own repo. [Likely]

### Next three moves

1. Owner action, ~15 minutes: make one final commit replacing README.md's body (and STATUS.md:5-11) with a pointer — 'Folded into skyhook on 2026-07-07 (skyhook commit 538ffc7 → docs/providers/README.md; develop-path model in finance/leg_cost.py + LEG_COST_FINDINGS.md + LEG_COST_REUSE_FINDINGS.md). Do not work here.' — then archive the GitHub repo (Settings → Archive). Prefer archive over delete so skyhook's citation at docs/providers/README.md:12 keeps resolving.
2. Before archiving, carry docs/decisions/TEMPLATE.md to wherever Aster family conventions will live (skyhook/docs/decisions/ does not exist yet; or a family-level conventions repo) — or consciously drop it. It is the only content in this repo that exists nowhere else.
3. In skyhook, finish what this repo was for: create docs/providers/rocket_lab_haste.md from MP-001 line 18 ($9.5M/flight contract-implied, 31 Aug 2026, secondary-sourced, spot-check pending) plus any primary source from the Rocket Lab reply; replace the unsourced ALPHA_PROCURE_USD_PER_FLIGHT at finance/leg_cost.py:29 with a tagged, sourced procure anchor; add the $87,986/catch reconciliation gate against rightsize_sensitivity.py and a pytest, so DECISION_LOG item 9's 'sourced pricing before anchor terms' gate is actually enforced.

### Time to v1

For this repo as a repo: not applicable — the recommended end state is 'archived with pointer', which is one ~15-minute sitting (one commit + GitHub archive toggle) [Certain]. For the MANDATE this repo was created to deliver (procure-vs-develop trade with sourced provider pricing), now living in skyhook: [Speculative] 3-5 weekend sessions of Hudson's time — 1-2 weekends for a primary-sourced provider landscape scan (Rocket Lab HASTE, Stoke, Dawn, others named in skyhook/docs/program_manifest.md:89) written one-file-per-provider with URLs and retrieval dates; 1 weekend to wire a sourced procure anchor into leg_cost.py, add the $87,986 reconciliation gate and a pytest; 1 weekend for the FLAGGED/UNSOURCED parameter verify pass (Isp, b_prod, Electron anchors per LEG_COST_REUSE_FINDINGS.md:20-28) and a findings doc. The schedule is gated on vendor replies that cannot be forced; if no provider will quote a ~4.6 km/s rendezvous leg, 'v1' becomes a documented 'no procure path exists' finding instead, which is faster (1-2 weekends) but changes the program's answer.

### Doc rot and false claims

- [Certain] This repo's STATUS.md:7-11 ('Suggested first: provider landscape scan ... Parked: trade-model skeleton ... reconciliation gate') and CLAUDE.md:24,27-28 ('create src/ when the first module lands', 'docs/providers/ create on first source') are stale directions: that work moved to skyhook on 2026-07-07 (commit 538ffc7) and nothing in this repo says so. Not false when written; misleading now.
- [Certain] skyhook/docs/providers/README.md:9-10 and :23-25 promise the trade model 'will ... follow [rightsize_sensitivity.py's] reconciliation-gate discipline' and 'must reproduce the per-catch marginal anchor (~$87,986/catch) before its findings are trusted'. finance/leg_cost.py contains no such gate (grep for 87986 across skyhook hits only RIGHTSIZE_FINDINGS.md and the providers README) and has no test; export_grid.py:296 calls it 'a separate, un-integrated model'.
- [Certain] skyhook/finance/LEG_COST_FINDINGS.md:66 and LEG_COST_REUSE_FINDINGS.md:5 cite 'handoff Section 6 #2' for the pending verify pass; skyhook/handoff.md was first committed 2026-09-02 (eac28a6), is a 31 Aug 2026 session handoff, and contains no mention of 'leg'. The reference resolves to nothing in the repo. [Likely] it pointed at an out-of-repo chat handoff.
- [Certain] skyhook/finance/leg_cost.py:29 `ALPHA_PROCURE_USD_PER_FLIGHT = 15_000_000.0` carries no source comment, yet Table B labels it 'vs $15M Alpha' as if it were a procurement price. This violates both this repo's CLAUDE.md:15-16 ('Every provider price cites a primary source and a retrieval date') and skyhook's Rule 5 (no magic numbers).
- [Certain] skyhook/docs/providers/README.md is orphaned: no other file in skyhook references docs/providers (grep exit 1), including skyhook/CLAUDE.md's file-location table and docs/program_manifest.md. The 'First task (not started)' at line 27 has been accurate for 3.5 months.
- [Certain, out of this card's strict scope but found while verifying] skyhook/docs/program_manifest.md:33 references `claude/skyhook_research_notes_2026-08.md` (RN-001, 'HASTE grade-A price'); no `claude/` directory or file of that name exists in the skyhook checkout.

### Review deviation notes

No file in any repo was created, edited, moved or deleted; no git write command was run; no scratchpad file was needed. This repo has nothing to run (no code, no tests), so no test suite was executed here and its CLAUDE.md 'verify before done' rule was satisfied trivially. Deviation: to measure the briefing's claim that the mandate now lives in skyhook, I executed `python3 finance/leg_cost.py` inside /home/user/skyhook at HEAD 34935cb (authorized by the task's read-only measurement allowance). That script's only side effect is a read-only `git rev-parse --short HEAD` (leg_cost.py:68); `git status --short` was empty both before and after the run, so no tracked file changed (only gitignored __pycache__ may have been touched). I did not run skyhook's pytest suite, since skyhook is another card's scope. I also issued read-only `gh api` GET calls against the GitHub REST API for this repo's metadata (archived flag, pushed_at, branches, commit count). `gh repo view` failed with HTTP 403 (GraphQL unavailable in this session) and was replaced with REST calls.

### 3.3 Overlaps and redundancies

- Three evidence-envelope implementations of one idea: Aster EvidenceEnvelope (mcp/src/envelope/index.ts, envelope-schema.ts), PartForge headless envelope (headless/partforge.mjs:3-5), skyhook export_grid provenance block (export_grid.py:97-113) — plus Idea-Bank's plain-file equivalents (72 belt-data.js header, 68 SOURCE.md). Same fields under different names; none validates against another's. [Certain]
- Two agent surfaces at 0.1.0 with two untested hosting stories: aster-mission-mcp (stdio, published, stale, uncompilable at HEAD) and PartForge headless/mcp.mjs + mcp-http.mjs + deploy/ (Streamable HTTP written, not deployed). Master plan §2 S3 (:21) deferred 'one server vs many' until lens #2 needed tools; lens #2 arrived from outside the plan. [Certain]
- Two decision-record formats and an orphaned third: Aster slice founding docs (DEC/OQ ids, LOCKED), skyhook DECISION_LOG + DEVIATIONS (append-only items, EXECUTED/OVERRIDDEN), and suborbital docs/decisions/TEMPLATE.md (DRAFT→PROPOSED→LOCKED, :15-16) used by nobody; skyhook has no docs/decisions/ at all. [Certain]
- Five hand-maintained living-state files, four of them stale or false at HEAD (Aster STATUS.md:12,20-27,339,484-486; skyhook handoff.md dated 31 Aug and silent on 46 branch commits; PartForge docs/STATUS.md:3,84-85,222-224; MORNING_REPORT.md reads as current). Only Idea-Bank's generated ledger is current. [Certain]

### 3.5 Biggest cross-repo risk

Agent-authored volume has outrun owner review and the repos' own single-source-of-truth rules in every live repo at once, and the family's only asset — verifiable honesty — is enforced by prose where the machines are silent. Concretely, in the four days 2026-10-22 → 10-26: skyhook received 46 noreply@anthropic.com commits (~12,000 lines) on six branches that no file in the repo mentions and CI never ran (ci.yml:4-7); Aster's origin received 7 'Claude' commits on three branches (k2-momentum-bank, ux-run-2026-10-22, aster-family) while AGENTS.md:103 says agents never push and the hooks that would stop it are inert in any fresh clone (100644, no hooksPath, no prepare); Idea-Bank's 108 commits (98 'Claude') landed in three days with 30 review commits now pointing at the wrong idea numbers; PartForge's 85/94 agent commits ship straight to a Pages-served main with no CI, and one deploy already failed silently. The same mechanism has already fired on the honesty layer itself: Aster's agent surface has been uncompilable since 2026-10-14 while 97 test files stayed green; CI on main went red on 2026-10-24 for a history-hygiene reason; skyhook's handoff.md, program_manifest.md and the rotovator-rev-a skill each assert facts false at HEAD; and the brand-new family hub (aster-family/) misstates two sibling repos within hours of its creation (MANIFEST.json:12,74,78; CONVENTIONS.md:27). The cross-repo form of this risk is the dangerous one: a family index, a shared skill or a snapshot that quotes a sibling from memory becomes the drift vector that carries one repo's error into four (DECISIONS_2026-07-07.md:16 '3.50 km/s'; SKILL.md:27 '440×'; master plan :41 'constants locked in its own skill'). If the bare name Aster is then stamped across all repos before clearance (MEDIUM conflict risk; ASTER Labs Inc. in the same field), the brand that is supposed to mean 'every number traces' would be attached to surfaces that demonstrably do not. Mitigation is already in the family's own toolbox: K2's hash-pinned snapshot + verify gate for every cross-repo fact; CI on every compiled or deployed surface; generated (not typed) state headers; and a per-repo push-policy ruling written down. [Certain] on every fact above; [Likely] on the consequence that reviewers, Fellows programs or a professor discover the gap before Hudson closes it.

### 3.6 What already exists for a family

More exists than the five repos' cross-references suggest (zero code links; `git grep` for Aster/skyhook/PartForge/Idea-Bank across siblings → 0 files in every direction except the Aster hub branch). All of it sits inside asteroid-mining-planner and all of it is leads-grade by its own labels.

(1) strategy/ASTER_FAMILY_MASTER_PLAN.md (drafted 2026-07-06; :3 'leads-grade; nothing here locks a DEC'; :4 supersedes the aster-complements-strategy skill's product list). §1 :9-15 states the conglomerate thesis (evidence-first decision layer; house line 'if the system cannot explain why it ranked … it does not present the output as strong'). §2 :17-24 names the substrate the family would actually own: S1 Evidence Core (EvidenceEnvelope v1), S2 Data Spine (41,906-body catalog, ephemeris core), S3 Agent Interface (aster-mcp), S4 Validation identity. §3 :30-44 maps twelve lenses with maturity states — L1 Transit SHIPPED (:33), P0 aster-mcp FOUNDED (:32), L2 Prospect shipped-in-core, L3 Dossier/L4 Ledger CHARTERED, L5-L9 CONCEPT, L10-L12 BACKLOG — and :41 defines L9 Momentum as the lens that 'connects the rotovator program'. §4 :52-61 is the market-honesty table (flagship 'Buyer today? No [Certain, ~]' :54; Traffic 'YES — the nearest real market' :58). §6 :73-77 sets gated waves (Wave 1 'Realistic: 1–2 of the three by end of 2026' :74 — none chartered as of today). §7 :83-88 holds the mechanics: chartering lifecycle CONCEPT→CHARTERED→FOUNDED→BUILDING→SHIPPED→SUNSET (:83), brand architecture 'Aster <one noun>' (:84), house positioning lines (:85), entity form 'none yet, deliberately' with explicit incorporation triggers (:86), and the repo policy that already answers the monorepo question (:87). §8 :92 names sprawl the death mode.

(2) strategy/ASTER_FAMILY_CHARTERS.md (2026-07-21; additive-only :3; routing rule :5 'sub-projects have no repo access'). Template §0 :8-20 (lens, decision question, entry gate, kill criteria, substrate reuse, new-domain import, market class, provenance). Four entries — Ledger, Survey, Traffic, Bench — all 'CHARTER PENDING' (:26, :32, :38, :44) under a §1 heading that says 'CHARTERED (Wave 1)' (:22). [Certain] This is the only place with a kill-criteria field, and it is empty.

(3) strategy/IDEA_KERNELS_2026-09.md (2026-09-11, Nova triage; additive §6). Twelve kernels survive 25 ideas (:46). K1 return-leg porkchop (:22; shortlist item 2 at :62-65: 'the prerequisite to K2 … links Hudson's two flagship projects through one well-defined interface: Earth-arrival v∞'). How the five repos map onto it. Aster = the substrate (S1-S4) + L1 Transit SHIPPED + P0 aster-mcp (FOUNDED, currently broken at HEAD) + L2 Prospect-in-core: the mothership and the only repo the plan was written about. Skyhook = the physical program behind L9 Momentum (:41); the plan imagines L9 as a planning TOOL consuming 'tether dynamics — HIGH' import, while the skyhook repo IS the tether-dynamics and economics study — so skyhook is a Works member and L9 the lens over it, and K2 is the first interface between them. suborbital = a sub-program of skyhook, already folded (MANIFEST.json integrations :211-218 records it). PartForge = no lens; a fabrication tool with its own envelope and MCP; fits the family as a Works member and as the measurement authority the ST-001 field kit and Idea-Bank's printables need. Idea-Bank = no lens; a public sandbox whose six space entries (03, 27, 41, 42, 72, 73) are the natural teaching layer for L12 Atlas (BACKLOG, :44) and whose tooling (smoke.mjs, printcheck.py, gallery.mjs, words tests) is the most reusable in the family. Two of five repos postdate the plan (PartForge 2026-08-26, Idea-Bank 2026-10-22), none of the four charters has an entry, and no member has adopted a convention — so what exists is a complete map, a working cross-repo mechanism and a proposed hub, with zero adoption. [Certain]

Method note for this synthesis step: I read the files above and ran only git show/log/ls-tree/status, cat, sed, grep and ls; no tests, no installs, no network, no writes. Final `git status --short`: skyhook (34935cb), Idea-Bank (1e5b7c6), Partforge (0974a62), suborbital (9e6eef3) clean; asteroid-mining-planner shows only the pre-existing ' M .dispatch-scope' on branch aster-family @ f077390 (main = origin/main = 62ddb17).
